### [1] [Stochastic Spatial Metapopulation Modelling of HPAI Control and Poultry Restocking on Jolly Island](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12956)
*Hammed O. Fatoyinbo,Indranil Ghosh,Parul Tiwari,Peter O. Olanipekun,Afeez Abidemi,Ryan H. L. Ip*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个随机空间SEIR元种群模型，模拟高度致病性禽流感（HPAI）在虚构Jolly岛上的暴发，整合了多种传播途径、防控策略（反应性和预防性扑杀、生产系统特异性隔离）以及基于容量的补栏策略，评估了不同干预措施对疫情负担和反弹风险的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPAI暴发需要快速控制疫情蔓延，同时需基于证据决定被清群农场的安全补栏时机。现有研究往往将疫情控制与恢复阶段分开处理，缺乏在同一建模框架内整合两者动态的综合性工具。

Method: 构建了随机空间SEIR元种群模型，应用于虚构的Jolly岛。农场分为"Broiler-2"、有机鸭和"Other"三种生产系统。模型整合了本地、环境、移动介导和距离依赖性传播途径，结合反应性和预防性扑杀、生产特异性隔离以及基于容量的补栏策略。通过模拟评估了预防性扑杀、提前隔离、环境传播强度及不同补栏方案（包括基于容量vs基线种群比例的补栏）对累计感染农场天数和反弹概率的影响。

Result: 预防性扑杀将平均累计负担从16,362.7降至13,631.9感染农场日，整体减少16.7%。提前隔离显著降低疫情规模，更强的环境传播则增加疫情峰值。补栏风险随疫情接近尾声而下降；在模型假设下，2026年5月24日是第一个满足反弹概率阈值0.20的候选日期，而2026年3月15日补栏均未达标。基于容量的补栏使累计负担降低8.45%，反弹概率从0.780降至0.533（相对基线种群比例补栏）。

Conclusion: 将疫情控制与疫情后恢复整合于单一建模框架具有重要价值。及时隔离、精准预防性扑杀和分阶段基于容量的补栏可同时降低疫情负担和反弹风险，但实际操作决策还需结合监测、生物安全、经济因素及监管要求。

Abstract: Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) outbreaks require rapid control during active transmission and evidence-based decisions on the safe restocking of depopulated farms. We developed a stochastic spatial SEIR-based metapopulation model for a synthetic HPAI outbreak on the fictional Jolly Island. Farms were classified as `Broiler-2', `organic duck', or `Other' production systems. The model incorporated local, environmental, movement-mediated, and distance-dependent transmission, together with reactive and preventive culling, production-specific confinement, and capacity-based restocking. The simulated epidemic was geographically concentrated and differed substantially among production classes. Preventive culling reduced mean cumulative burden from 16,362.7 to 13,631.9 infectious-farm-days, with an overall reduction of 16.7\%. Earlier confinement substantially reduced epidemic magnitude, while stronger environmental transmission increased the epidemic peak. Restocking risk declined as the epidemic approached resolution. Under the model assumptions, 24 May 2026 was the first candidate date satisfying the predefined rebound-probability threshold of 0.20. For restocking on 15 March 2026, none of the tested restocking fractions met this criterion. Capacity-based restocking reduced cumulative burden by 8.45\% and rebound probability from 0.780 to 0.533, compared with restocking relative to the baseline population. These findings demonstrate the value of integrating epidemic control and post-outbreak recovery within a single modelling framework. Timely confinement, targeted preventive culling, and phased capacity-based restocking may reduce both epidemic burden and resurgence risk, although operational decisions should also incorporate surveillance, biosecurity, economic considerations, and regulatory requirements.

</details>


### [2] [Shared environmental risk selects asymmetric inheritance of a protective reserve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13370)
*Quentin Thommen*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了环境共享如何改变多样化价值，进而驱动细胞对保守保护性储备资源的继承策略发生对称性破缺。通过将随机人口算子与Top Lyapunov指数关联，发现弱环境共享偏好对称继承，而充分共享的创新会选择离散的非对称分支（α≈0.2），转变通过有限分支跨越而非连续偏离发生。作者用主导模态二阶近似分离出非对称优势的代价与收益来源，该近似可预测全随机算子动力学选择出的非对称分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究环境共享（而非环境变化本身的边际统计）如何改变多样化价值，并揭示该效应能否在细胞对保守保护性储备资源的继承策略中驱动对称性破缺。关键疑点在于：即使每个谱系经历的边际统计保持不变，共享性本身是否足以改变最优继承策略。

Method: 构建最小化细胞分裂模型：每个母亲将固定保守储备量分配给两个女儿，每种固定分配策略对应一个随机人口算子，用Top Lyapunov指数刻画长期增长率。对比部分共享与私有环境创新（二者边际统计相同但共享性不同），使用主导模态二阶近似将非对称优势分解为"均算子的专业化增长代价"与"对集体波动敏感性的降低"两部分，并与全随机算子乘积的数值结果对照。

Result: 弱环境共享偏好对称继承；充分共享的创新会经有限分支跨越选择α≈0.2处的分离非对称分支，而非从等分连续偏离。改变保护律或储备周转时非对称相位持续存在，但其边界依赖于保护非线性与储备记忆。共享与私有创新的边际统计相同，因此均值生殖算子独立于共享创新比例；二阶近似可准确预测非对称分支，而数值跨越需全算子乘积确定。

Conclusion: 环境共享能够在谱系间多样化损失的情况下，提升在分裂时产生多样化的价值，从而驱动保守保护性资源继承策略的对称性破缺。该结果明确了环境共享本身（而非边际统计变化）可作为表型多态性涌现的驱动因素。

Abstract: Environmental sharing changes the value of diversification even when the marginal statistics experienced by each lineage remain unchanged. A minimal model of cell division couples this effect to the inheritance of a conserved protective reserve. Each mother partitions its reserve between two daughters, and each fixed partition policy generates a random demographic operator whose top Lyapunov exponent determines long-term growth. Weak environmental sharing favors symmetric inheritance, whereas sufficiently shared innovation selects a separated asymmetric branch near {α\simeq 0.2}. The transition therefore occurs by a finite branch crossing rather than by a continuous departure from equal partition. The asymmetric phase persists when the protection law or reserve turnover is changed, although its boundary depends on protection nonlinearity and reserve memory. Because shared and private environmental innovations have identical marginal statistics, the mean reproductive operator is independent of the shared-innovation fraction. A dominant-mode second-order approximation then separates the asymmetric advantage into a mean-operator growth cost of specialization and a reduction of sensitivity to collective fluctuations. This approximation predicts the finite asymmetric branch selected by the full random-operator dynamics, while the full operator product determines the numerical crossing. Environmental sharing can therefore drive symmetry breaking in the inheritance of a conserved protective resource when the loss of diversification between lineages increases the value of diversification generated at division.

</details>

### [3] [RoboSynChallenge: Mastering Real-World Dexterity via Generalizing Synthesized Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12416)
*Runyi Zhao,Ruixin Wu,Chengkun Li,Hongrui Zhang,Ang Li,Ruixing Jin,Yueci Deng,Yingying Guo,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Tianfan Xue,Yanjun Gao,Yudong Luo,Pascal Poupart,Simo Wu,Kui Jia,Wei-shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSynChallenge是一个统一基准测试平台，通过大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实世界机器人评估相结合的方式，解决机器人操作策略泛化能力不足和真实数据稀缺的问题，推动通用操作智能的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作泛化是具身智能的核心挑战。尽管模型架构和学习算法不断进步，但真实世界数据的稀缺性和多样性不足严重限制了策略的泛化能力，因此需要一个统一的基准来评估和推动操作策略在多种任务、环境和难度下的泛化性能。

Method: 该方法通过两条核心路径构建：(1) 集成大规模合成数据生成，利用状态-动作轨迹缓解真实数据短缺问题；(2) 仅在未见的真实世界操作环境中进行最终评估，确保泛化能力的严格验证。同时提供Transformer、Diffusion、VLA和World-Action-Model等多种基线实现以保证可复现性和可比性。

Result: RoboSynChallenge将可扩展的仿真训练与严谨的真实世界验证相结合，为参与者提供标准化评估框架和多类基线模型，旨在鼓励研发数据高效、广泛适应能力的操作策略，从而推动通用机器人智能的发展。

Conclusion: 通过耦合仿真训练与真实世界验证，RoboSynChallenge为攻克机器人操作泛化难题提供了可行的基准平台，有望促进具备广泛能力、数据高效且自适应的操作系统的诞生，进而迈向真正通用的机器人智能。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable robotic manipulation remains a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Despite rapid advances in model architectures and learning algorithms, progress is often limited by the scarcity and narrow diversity of real-world data. The RoboSynChallenge competition introduces a unified benchmark to evaluate and advance the generalizability of manipulation policies across a spectrum of tasks, environments, and difficulty levels. To alleviate the shortage of realistic data, the challenge integrates large-scale synthetic data generation with standardized real-world robotic evaluation. Participants are encouraged to leverage synthesized state-action trials to improve general-purpose policy learning, while final assessments are conducted exclusively on unseen real-world manipulation environments. Baseline implementations, including Transformer-, Diffusion-, Vision-Language-Action, and World-Action-Model-based policies, are provided to ensure reproducibility and comparability. By coupling scalable simulation-based training with rigorous real-world validation, RoboSynChallenge aims to foster the development of broadly capable, data-efficient, and adaptable manipulation systems, thereby paving the way toward truly general robotic intelligence.

</details>


### [4] [Attune: A Self-Annotation Tool for Understanding Robot Operator Attention Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12650)
*Puqi Zhou,Sungsoo Ray Hong,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Attune工具，一种部署前引出工具，通过自动识别操作员眼神注视转移、提供AI辅助标注并输出注视模式摘要，帮助多机器人监控界面设计者理解并校准机器人行为以捕获、维持或放弃操作员注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂的真实环境中部署机器人编队需要人类操作员同时监督多个机器人。管理操作员注意力是多机器人监督界面设计的基础挑战，但目前设计者缺乏关于如何通过改变机器人行为来捕获、维持或放弃操作员注意力的经验性指导。

Method: 将操作员眼神注视作为机器人行为设计线索，创建了部署前引出工具Attune。该工具自动识别有意义的注视转移，提供AI辅助标注注视转移原因，并输出操作员注视模式摘要供其审查。通过用户研究让参与者标注吸引其注意力的视觉触发因素来评估工具。

Result: 研究结果揭示了观察到的注视模式的变化，并展示了Attune如何帮助表征操作员注意力。

Conclusion: Attune能够帮助设计者通过操作员注视模式理解注意力的分布和转移，为多机器人监督界面中机器人行为设计提供经验性依据，使设计者可以针对不同操作员注意力特征来校准机器人行为。

Abstract: Deploying robot fleets in complex, real-world environments requires human operators to supervise multiple robots simultaneously. Managing operator attention is a fundamental challenge of designing multi-robot supervision interfaces, encompassing both feed layout and feed content (i.e., robot behavior design). Thus far, designers lack empirical guidance on the latter-how to change a robot's behavior to capture, sustain, or relinquish operator attention during multi-robot supervision. In our vision of the future, designers should be able to use this guidance to calibrate robot behavior to different operator attention profiles. Treating operator eye gaze as a robot behavior design clue, we created a pre-deployment elicitation tool called Attune. Attune automatically identifies when meaningful gaze shifts occur, provides AI assistance for annotating why shifts occurred, and outputs a summary of operator gaze patterns for operator review. We evaluated Attune through a user study in which participants annotated the visual triggers that drew their attention. Our findings unveil variation in observed gaze patterns and reveal how Attune helps characterize operator attention.

</details>


### [5] [FUSE: Active Functional Affordance Grounding through Adaptive Semantic-Geometric Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12683)
*Zhou Chen,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动功能可供性定位（Active Functional Affordance Grounding）任务，以及一个名为FUSE的自适应语义-几何证据采集框架，使具身智能体能够通过主动探索场景、选择信息量最大的视角来识别并空间定位满足功能查询的对象。实验表明FUSE在非Oracle条件下取得最优定位性能，且计算效率比完全显式探索提升1.33倍，并兼容多种可供性知识源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体常需基于功能而非身份来识别和交互对象，但现有可供性定位方法仅从固定视角工作，缺乏在功能线索被遮挡或不完整时决定"往哪里看"的机制，这限制了其在真实场景中的适应性。

Method: 提出FUSE框架，结合显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习的摊销规划器（amortized planner），实现高效的视角选择；同时构建基于Habitat的基准，用于评估主动功能定位任务，并支持多种可供性知识源。

Result: FUSE在非Oracle条件下取得最高定位性能，计算量相较完全显式探索降低1.33倍，且在不同可供性知识源下均保持有效。

Conclusion: 主动探索策略对于功能驱动的对象定位至关重要，FUSE通过显式与摊销规划的结合在性能与效率之间取得了良好平衡，为具身智能体的功能导向交互提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents must often identify and interact with objects based on their function rather than their identity, requiring them to actively acquire observations that reveal discriminative functional evidence. Existing affordance grounding methods operate from fixed viewpoints and lack mechanisms for deciding where to look when functional cues are occluded or incomplete. We introduce Active Functional Affordance Grounding, a new task in which an agent sequentially explores a scene to identify and spatially ground an object satisfying a functional query. To address this problem, we propose FUSE, an adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework that combines explicit uncertainty-driven exploration with a learned amortized planner to efficiently select informative viewpoints. We further introduce a Habitat-based benchmark for evaluating active functional grounding. Experiments show that FUSE achieves the highest observed non-oracle grounding performance while reducing computation by 1.33x relative to fully explicit exploration, and remains effective across multiple affordance knowledge sources.

</details>


### [6] [SAP-Nav: Spatial Semantic Representation Meets Active Perception for Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12707)
*Xuetong Pei,Jian Liu,Vidura Munasinghe,Bo Miao,U-Xuan Tan,Wenrui Ding,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAP-Nav是一个面向层次化开放词汇物体导航（OVON）的全在线、零样本框架，通过主动感知增量构建可查询的空间语义表示，并采用主动视点验证机制，在部分观测环境下实现对场景、房间、区域和实例级目标指令的可靠导航，在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上取得最优性能，并在真实机器人实验中验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化OVON要求智能体在未知环境中遵循自由形式指令，这些指令可能通过场景、房间、区域和实例级别的线索指定目标。尽管LangMap已正式定义这一设定，但在部分观测条件下可靠解决仍具挑战性：空间定位需要持久的整体环境证据，而目标验证需要清晰且具有判别力的候选视角。现有方法无法同时满足这两个需求。

Method: SAP-Nav通过主动感知解决上述两大需求：1）从主动获取的房间视角增量构建可查询的空间语义表示（Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation），支持从任意已探索位置进行空间语义查询；2）采用主动视点验证（Active Viewpoint Verification）机制，评估当前观测是否提供充分证据，必要时将智能体重新定位到更具信息量的视点，再依据类别和属性约束验证目标候选。

Result: 在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上，SAP-Nav取得整体最优性能，其中在区域级导航上相比基于训练的方法SR提升12.2%。真实机器人实验进一步验证了其实际可行性。

Conclusion: SAP-Nav作为一个无需任务特定训练或预计算场景地图的零样本框架，能够同时支持层次化OVON和标准类别级OVON，通过主动感知有效解决了部分观测下空间语义定位与目标验证的矛盾，展示了在该任务上的最佳性能与真实部署潜力。

Abstract: Hierarchical open-vocabulary object navigation (OVON) requires agents to follow free-form instructions that may specify targets through scene-, room-, region-, and instance-level cues in unseen environments. Although recent work LangMap has formalized this setting, reliably solving it under partial observations remains challenging: spatial grounding requires persistent environment-level evidence, whereas target verification requires clear and discriminative candidate views. We present SAP-Nav, a fully online, zero-shot framework that addresses both requirements through active perception. SAP-Nav incrementally constructs a Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation from actively acquired room views, enabling spatial semantic queries from any explored location. It further employs Active Viewpoint Verification to assess whether the current observation provides sufficient evidence and, when necessary, reposition the agent to a more informative viewpoint before verifying candidates against category and attribute constraints. Although designed for hierarchical OVON, SAP-Nav supports both hierarchical and standard category-level OVON without task-specific training or precomputed scene maps. Experiments on LangMap and HM3D-OVON show that SAP-Nav achieves the overall best performance, including a 12.2% improvement in SR over training-based methods on region-level navigation. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate its practical feasibility. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [7] [Genetic Fuzzy System-Based Multi-Robot Coordination for Planetary Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12755)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于遗传模糊系统的分散式多机器人协同物体搬运方法，通过地形可通行性分析将高程图转换为二维可通行性地图，并利用遗传算法优化模糊推理系统，在非结构化环境中最小化总路径长度并避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在非结构化环境中执行协同物体搬运任务时，面临路径长度优化与避障的挑战，需要一种能适应复杂地形（如斜坡、障碍物密集区域）的分散式控制策略。

Method: 首先基于高程图进行地形坡度可通行性分析，识别不可通行区域并转换为二维可通行性地图；其次设计多个模糊推理系统（FISs）用于生成机器人搬运物体的速度；最后采用遗传算法在多种场景（局部极小值、目标靠近障碍物、杂乱环境）下优化FIS参数。

Result: 训练后的FIS模型应用于测试环境（转换后的可通行性地图），并在多个场景中进行了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的分散式遗传模糊系统方法能够在非结构化环境中有效实现多机器人协同搬运任务，在最小化总路径长度的同时实现避障。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [8] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AirForesight框架，通过当前到未来的空间地图想象（current-to-future spatial map imagination）来解决无人机视觉-语言导航（UAV-VLN）中缺乏显式场景grounding和未来感知推理的问题，在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法通常将视觉-语言输入直接映射到动作，缺少显式的场景grounding和具有未来感知能力的空间推理，难以应对复杂户外环境中稀疏多视角观测带来的空间理解挑战。

Method: AirForesight框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从多视角观测中学习结构化当前地图表示，通过当前地图重建和未来轨迹预测联合监督；(2) 在结构化因果注意力（structured causal attention）下将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理；(3) 引入跨空间规划一致性损失（cross-space planning consistency loss），促使预测的地图空间轨迹方向与基于真值航点位移的专家动作方向一致，最后聚合当前和未来表示来预测下一个3D航点。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S两个基准数据集上的实验以及大量消融研究，验证了框架的强性能和有效性、稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模当前场景结构并推理未来空间地图，AirForesight有效提升了UAV-VLN中Agent的场景理解和未来感知能力，为复杂户外环境下的无人机自主导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning. We propose AirForesight, a current-to-future spatial map imagination framework for UAV-VLN. AirForesight first learns a structured current-map representation from multi-view observations. This representation is jointly supervised by current-map reconstruction and future-trajectory prediction, encouraging it to encode both present scene structure and future motion intent. Under structured causal attention, the current spatial knowledge is propagated to future-map reasoning, and the resulting current and future representations are aggregated to predict the next 3D waypoint. To make spatial imagination more relevant to navigation, we introduce a cross-space planning consistency loss that encourages directional agreement between the predicted map-space trajectory and the expert action direction derived from the ground-truth waypoint displacement. Experiments on OpenUAV and AerialVLN-S, together with extensive ablations, demonstrate strong performance and support the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [9] [ASPIRE-VINS: Adaptive Spline-based Visual-inertial Navigation System With Robust 3D Measurement Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12840)
*Kwangyik Jung,Eungchang Mason Lee,Taekjun Oh,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPIRE-VINS，一种自适应连续时间视觉惯性导航系统（VINS），通过自适应节点放置（AKP）、多分辨率样条（MRS）和3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR）相结合，实现了运动自适应的连续时间轨迹建模，在多种运动和传感条件下取得优于或持平基线方法的轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散时间VINS方法（如IMU预积分）精度和效率高，但基于关键帧的表示在需要任意时间戳残差评估或运动相关的时间分辨率要求时不够灵活。连续时间样条虽然解决了这一问题，但均匀分布的节点可能对快速动态段欠表示或对静态区间过参数化，造成精度或效率损失。

Method: ASPIRE-VINS由三个核心组件构成：（1）自适应节点放置（AKP），根据局部运动变化分配样条节点；（2）多分辨率样条（MRS），在切空间中添加有界局部细化以提升表达力；（3）3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR），通过将变换后的特征与校准观测射线在3D测量空间中对齐来实现方位一致性约束。

Result: 实验结果表明，ASPIRE-VINS在多种运动和传感条件下取得了与基线方法相比有竞争力或更低的轨迹误差，验证了运动自适应连续时间轨迹建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 运动自适应连续时间样条表示能够有效克服均匀节点在表达动态运动时的局限性，在保持连续时间框架灵活性的同时提升VINS的精度，为连续时间视觉惯性导航提供了一种更优的轨迹表示方案。

Abstract: Visual-inertial navigation systems estimate six-degree-of-freedom motion by fusing visual and inertial data. Modern discrete-time methods with IMU preintegration provide strong accuracy and efficiency, but keyframe-based representations can be less flexible when residuals must be evaluated at arbitrary timestamps or when motion-dependent temporal resolution is needed. Continuous-time splines address this issue by representing the trajectory as a smooth temporal function, but uniformly spaced knots can under-represent rapid dynamics or over-parameterize static intervals. This letter proposes ASPIRE-VINS, a continuous-time VINS framework that combines adaptive knot placement (AKP), multi-resolution splines (MRS), and 3D measurement-space residuals (3D-MSR). AKP allocates knots according to local motion variation, MRS adds bounded local refinement in tangent space, and 3D-MSR provides bearing consistency by aligning transformed features with calibrated observation rays in 3D measurement space. Experiments show that ASPIRE-VINS achieves competitive or lower trajectory errors than the compared baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-adaptive continuous-time trajectory modeling under diverse motion and sensing conditions.

</details>


### [10] [BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12854)
*Bing Zhan,Shuyao Shang,Jiahao Gu,Shuo Lu,Yuan Xu,Zhao Wang,Yida Wang,Xueyang Zhang,Kun Zhan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainWAM是一个统一的自动驾驶规划框架，通过结构化动作空间协调将语义推理（VLA）与预测性世界建模（WAM）结合，解决朴素组合中注意力分配不匹配的问题，在NAVSIM v1和v2上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规划需要同时满足语义约束和预测动力学，但现有的端到端方法（VLA模型和世界动作模型）各自只强调其中一方面。简单地将二者通过token级注意力联合会导致注意力分配不匹配——语义捷径主导共享注意力空间，抑制预测动力学。

Method: 受神经科学中复杂行为源于功能特化系统协调的启发，BrainWAM将语义推理和预测世界建模转化为两条特化的动作导向通路，在紧凑动作表征层面对齐二者。同时引入异步整流流（asynchronous rectified-flow）推理策略，解耦视频和动作去噪过程，降低推理延迟同时保留规划相关的预测上下文。

Result: BrainWAM在NAVSIM v1上达到89.5 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到89.6 EPDMS，均超越仅使用VLA或仅使用WAM的方法，达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: BrainWAM验证了结构化动作空间协调是融合语义先验与预测动力学的有效途径，为自动驾驶系统提供了一种实用且有前景的统一规划框架方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires planning under both semantic constraints and predictive dynamics. Existing end-to-end driving approaches, however, typically emphasize only one side of this requirement: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exploit VLM priors for semantic reasoning, while World Action Models (WAMs) provide future-aware prediction through generative world modeling. This naturally motivates a unified planner that can leverage both semantic priors and predictive dynamics. However, we find that a naive combination through joint token-level attention suffers from an attention-allocation mismatch, where semantic shortcuts dominate the shared attention space and suppress predictive dynamics. Inspired by neuroscience evidence that complex behavior arises from coordination among functionally specialized systems, we propose BrainWAM, a structured action-space coordination framework that converts semantic reasoning and predictive world modeling into two specialized action-oriented pathways, and aligns them at the level of compact action representations. We further introduce an asynchronous rectified-flow inference strategy with decoupled video and action denoising, which shortens inference latency while preserving planning-relevant predictive context. BrainWAM reaches state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM v1 (89.5 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (89.6 EPDMS), consistently outperforming VLA-only or WAM-only methods, highlighting BrainWAM as a practical and promising direction for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [11] [HumanoidVLN: A Physics-Grounded Simulator and Benchmark for Vision-Language Navigation Across Diverse Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12860)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,The-Anh Nguyen,Minh Nguyen-Dinh,Phuong Nam Dang,Tri Pham,Hung Tran,Bach Dao,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanoidVLN，一个基于物理的仿真器和基准，用于跨多种人形机器人形态的视觉语言导航。平台基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim，支持四种人形机器人（Unitree G1、H1等），通过分层控制栈结合强化学习运动策略和PD/MPC路径跟踪器。实验显示JanusVLN在四个模型和四种形态中取得最高平均成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38；在20个场景的sim-to-real测试中，导航误差与仿真强相关（r=0.935）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准未能解决人形机器人导航的三个关键挑战：双足运动带来的物理约束（轮式智能体不存在）、人形形态跨平台差异大、以及运动引起的相机动态导致第一人称观测畸变。缺乏一个物理感知、支持多种人形形态的仿真平台。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建物理仿真平台，支持四种人形机器人形态（10-12下肢自由度，身高1.17m至1.80m），采用分层控制栈：强化学习运动策略配合可互换的PD或MPC路径跟踪器。环境源自艺术家设计场景和3D高斯溅射重建，筛选导航面积超100平方米。指令由双生成器-评审者+释义器的多智能体流水线生成（含人在环验证），产出933条碰撞感知参考路径，每条配一个细粒度指令和三个粗粒度文体变体（正式、自然、口语化）。

Result: 在四种VLN模型（NaVILA、DualVLN、StreamVLN、JanusVLN）和四种人形形态上评测，JanusVLN取得最高平均成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38。20个场景的sim-to-real试点测试（DualVLN + Unitree G1）中，导航误差相关性r=0.935，平均绝对差0.68m，平均轨迹相似度nDTW为0.782（±0.188）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了VLN模型、控制器与人形形态在物理执行下的交互关系，为跨人形平台的视觉语言导航研究提供了可扩展的基准工具。代码、基准和数据将在论文接收后开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [12] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR-Pose，一种基于主动LED标记的AUV相对位姿估计框架，通过融合SE(3)李群位姿传播、概率标记关联和自适应可见性测量融合的PSwPnP估计器，实现水下复杂环境下的鲁棒六自由度相对位姿估计，并在水槽实验和闭环跟随实验中验证了其精度与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下机器人（AUV）之间可靠的相对位姿估计对协同海洋探索、采样和多机器人协调至关重要，但水下环境的严重光学退化（浑浊、光照变化、反射、特征间歇性遮挡）使得基于视觉的相对定位极具挑战性。

Method: 提出AMR-Pose框架，包括：(1) 设计由1个红色中心LED和3个蓝色外围LED组成的紧凑标记模块集成到领航AUV上，在复杂水下条件下提供显著视觉特征；(2) 开发概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），结合SE(3)上的李群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合，实现鲁棒六自由度相对位姿估计；(3) 动态适应标记可见性变化，在部分观测和可见性切换期间保持几何一致性和时间稳定性。

Result: 水槽实验中以运动捕捉系统提供地面真值，验证了AMR-Pose在挑战性水下条件下实现了准确、平滑且鲁棒的相对位姿估计；闭环领航-跟随实验进一步证明了其在协同水下机器人实时相对位姿反馈中的可行性。

Conclusion: AMR-Pose通过主动LED标记与概率切换PnP估计器的结合，有效解决了水下视觉退化和标记遮挡导致的相对位姿估计难题，为协同AUV系统提供了可靠、实时的相对定位方案。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [13] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal GRPO方法，通过构造可检测的任务阶段、对齐阶段特定的动作区间，并仅比较进入相同阶段的轨迹，解决VLA策略后训练中GRPO的轨迹级信用分配混淆问题。在RoboTwin 2.0和LIBERO-Long上取得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GRPO的VLA后训练中，一个rollout级优势被应用于轨迹中每个动作。当一条轨迹完成了多个有效阶段但最终失败时，之前产生进展的动作也会被惩罚，即"轨迹级信用混淆"（trajectory-level credit aliasing）。这一问题导致信用分配不准确，限制了策略学习的效率和效果。

Method: Temporal GRPO通过三个步骤解决信用混淆问题：(1) 构造可检测的任务阶段；(2) 将每条rollout与阶段特定的动作区间对齐；(3) 仅比较进入相同阶段的rollouts，将计算得到的阶段优势应用于对应的动作区间，并在单次策略更新中完成。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上，Temporal GRPO提升了任务成功率和样本效率，在不同任务长度上表现一致；在LIBERO-Long上，受控更新保留了共享的先决阶段，并将改进集中在rollout结果出现分歧的第一个阶段。

Conclusion: Temporal GRPO通过粒度更细的阶段级信用分配，有效解决了轨迹级信用混淆问题，在多个基准上带来一致的任务成功率和样本效率提升，是一种更精确的VLA策略后训练方法。

Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>


### [14] [H2R-Bench: Benchmarking Human-to-Robot Manipulation Video Generation in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13049)
*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了H2R-Bench基准，用于系统评估视频世界模型在跨具身（人类到机器人）操作视频生成中的表现。通过对11个SOTA视频生成模型在6个操作类别和2种机器人具身上的测试，发现现有模型在人类到机器人操作迁移中仍存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖大规模操作数据，但采集真实机器人示范数据昂贵且难以扩展。虽然人类第一视角操作视频蕴含丰富的行为经验，但人类手与机器人末端执行器之间的具身差异使得跨具身迁移困难。视频世界模型提供了一条从人类观察合成机器人视角操作视频的可能路径，但其跨具身迁移能力尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建H2R-Bench基准，将人类第一视角示范视频转换为特定具身条件下的机器人操作视频。每个基准实例包含人类示范视频、目标具身约束以及包括任务目标、动作事件、功能接触和物体响应在内的源接地标注。从目标状态完成度、动作事件完成度、功能接触迁移、具身正确性和视频质量五个维度评估生成视频。基准测试11个SOTA视频生成模型，覆盖6个操作类别和2种机器人具身。

Result: 评测显示当前视频世界模型在人类到机器人操作迁移方面仍能力有限：即使是领先模型在具身一致性、功能交互和任务执行方面也经常失败。H2R-Bench为系统的跨具身能力诊断提供了基准框架。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench提供了一个系统的诊断框架，用于评估视频世界模型能否弥合人类到机器人的具身鸿沟，并将人类操作观察转化为机器人中心的训练资源。当前模型仍无法很好地完成这一任务，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [15] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为具身智能体的空间推理模拟器。SRF通过将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割结果提升到3D辐射场中，统一编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份，从而在保持真实外观的同时提供语义真值，支持新颖视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，并展示了在果园苹果采摘任务中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练和评估具身智能体的空间推理能力需要既几何保真又支持语义查询的多样化环境。现有的合成模拟器虽然具有语义真值但缺乏真实感，基于真实世界重建的模拟器虽有真实外观但默认缺乏语义真值。作者试图弥合这两类模拟器之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出语义辐射场（SRF）表示方法：从带姿态的真实场景RGB采集数据出发，将预训练视觉模型产生的2D语义分割结果提升至3D辐射场中，联合编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份。该表示支持新颖视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，并通过物理引擎获得占用查询以支持交互。作为示例应用，构建了基于SRF的果园苹果采摘任务模拟器。

Result: SRF实现了从真实场景构建既具有真实外观又具备语义真值的模拟环境的能力，高效生成多样化真实世界环境来训练和评估空间推理模型。在果园苹果采摘任务中，辐射场同时提供相机渲染、语义真值和物理引擎所需的占用查询。

Conclusion: 语义辐射场（SRF）是一种统一的表示框架，能够在单个grounded表示中同时编码几何、外观和语义，从而为空间推理智能体提供真实且语义可查询的训练与评估环境，弥补了合成模拟器与真实重建模拟器之间的不足。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [16] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出S2-HWM（稀疏事件结构化层次世界模型），通过学习原始潜轨迹中的稀疏事件证据，协调事件级管理器与原始步工作者，实现长时程外科手术机器人操控。在SurRoL PegTransfer任务上达到98.7%的成功率，比平铺GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程外科手术机器人操控面临奖励稀疏且有意义交互变化间隔不规则的挑战。现有世界模型以原始步分辨率进行想象，无法显式捕捉可变时长的任务进展；手工指定的阶段边界难以与状态依赖的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM包含三个关键组件：(1) 从原始潜轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，用于调度事件级管理器的目标更新；(2) 每个选定的潜目标条件化工作者的原始动作直到下一次更新；(3) 事件证据构成可变时长分段，用于事件转换模型（ETM）预测下一边界随机状态、分段持续时间和累积分段奖励。事件级预测链式连接提供超出原始想象视野的可变时长延续用于管理器学习，工作者保留原始步actor-critic学习。

Result: 在SurRoL PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM达到98.7%的成功率，相较平铺GAS DreamerV3基线提升22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过引入稀疏事件驱动的层次化世界模型结构，有效解决了长时程操控中奖励稀疏与事件间隔不规则的问题，验证了事件级抽象在机器人操控学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [17] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FAM-DQ，一种基于双四旋翼的 fully actuated 空中机械臂，通过将两个四旋翼推进模块安装在中央框架两端并经被动关节连接、以齿轮驱动舵机调节指向方向，实现解耦的六自由度末端执行器控制与全方位操作能力。实验验证了轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和螺丝驱动等任务，最大扭矩1.019 N·m，总质量0.447 kg，扭矩质量比2.28 N·m/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欠驱动空中机械臂存在位置-姿态强耦合问题，而 fully actuated 平台设计往往面临结构复杂、有效载荷有限和扭矩输出不足等挑战，难以满足空中物理交互所需的大交互力和精确末端控制。

Method: 采用双四旋翼推进模块布置于中央框架两端，通过被动关节连接，并利用齿轮驱动舵机调节指向方向，从而在减轻结构复杂度的同时实现解耦的6自由度末端执行器控制、全方位操作能力和增强的扭矩输出。

Result: FAM-DQ在轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪等实验中验证了设计的可行性，最大扭矩达1.019 N·m，总质量仅0.447 kg，扭矩质量比达到2.28 N·m/kg。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ通过双四旋翼加被动关节+齿轮舵机的构型设计，在保持相对轻量（0.447 kg）的同时获得了高扭矩输出（1.019 N·m）和解耦的6自由度控制，为高扭矩空中物理交互任务提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [18] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究对制造复杂软体气动执行器的多种制造工艺（热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末/液体基增材制造及FDM）进行了系统评估，提出了面向制造的设计方法，其中FDM因其缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除而被认为最具适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软体气动执行器的制造面临几何保真度、柔顺性、结构完整性和气密性需同时满足的挑战，缺乏系统的工艺选型指导。

Method: 采用工艺筛选、基线制造、失效分析和工艺改进四步评估框架，比较热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末基/液体基增材制造和FDM等多种制造路线，区分固有工艺局限与可修正的制造缺陷。

Result: 热缩成型受几何贴合度限制，浇铸受模具可达性和粘接界面限制，粉末基方法受密闭通道内残余材料限制，数字光处理受材料性能和后处理要求限制；FDM最具适应性且其主导缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除。气密性不仅取决于名义壁厚还取决于挤出路径架构，无支撑几何在内部后处理受限时至关重要。

Conclusion: 建立了实用的面向制造的设计方法，以执行器架构与制造约束的兼容性指导工艺选择，为开发复杂、柔顺且气密的软体气动执行器提供实践指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [19] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级模块化框架，直接在末端执行器速度指令层面实现主动碰撞避免。通过基于碰撞时间（TTC）的预测模块和Rodrigues旋转公式偏转相对速度向量，该方法在保留速度幅值的同时改变方向，解决了传统人工势场方法的死锁问题，并在仿真和物理实验中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作使机器人能够执行类人灵巧运动，但操作员在遇到突发障碍时（尤其存在网络延迟或注意力有限的情况下）可能无法及时有效地规避障碍，造成安全风险。现有方法（如APF）存在死锁问题，需要一种轻量级的、可在速度指令层面直接生效的主动避障方案。

Method: 框架包含两个核心模块：1）预测模块：对点云进行预处理后，基于碰撞时间（TTC）并集成超调保护来预测潜在碰撞，同时补偿复杂遥操作管线中引入的点云处理延迟；2）偏转模块：利用Rodrigues旋转公式旋转相对速度向量，仅改变其方向而保持幅值不变，从而避免APF方法常见的死锁问题。该框架在末端执行器速度指令层面直接运行，设计轻量以支持高频控制。

Result: 在多种仿真场景中，所提方法比基线方法取得了更高的末端执行器碰撞避免率；物理机器人系统实验进一步验证了其避障的有效性。

Conclusion: 该轻量级模块化框架在速度指令层面实现了有效的主动碰撞避免，兼具高频率控制和延迟补偿能力，为遥操作系统提供了实用的安全增强方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [20] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NestDex是一个嵌套式策略学习框架，通过让操作者控制机械臂并通过单自由度离合器调节手部技能而非直接指定手指轨迹，降低了灵巧操作演示采集的难度。内层手部策略利用本体感觉历史信息自适应运动，视觉-语言选择器为各任务阶段激活合适技能；生成的演示用于训练独立的外层视觉运动策略，部署时无需内层策略。手部动作变分自编码器提供紧凑的手部动作目标，同时保留关节空间的机械臂命令。真实世界实验表明NestDex提升了演示可靠性和效率，支持有效的自主策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作（dexterous manipulation）有望极大地丰富机器人与物理世界的交互能力，但学习这些行为受限于采集一致、完整的全任务演示的困难。与平行夹爪操作不同，灵巧任务要求操作者在整个任务过程中协调机械臂运动与精确的、接触丰富的手指行为，这使得直接指定完整手指轨迹进行演示采集变得极其困难。

Method: NestDex采用嵌套式策略学习框架：(1) 内层手部策略根据最新的本体感觉历史信息自适应调整其运动，操作者通过单自由度离合器调节手部技能，而非直接指定完整手指轨迹；(2) 视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活合适的技能；(3) 生成的演示用于训练一个独立的外层视觉运动策略，该策略在部署时同时控制机械臂和手部，无需内层策略参与；(4) 手部动作变分自编码器（hand-action VAE）提供紧凑的手部动作目标，同时保留关节空间中的机械臂命令。

Result: 在真实世界的灵巧操作实验中，NestDex提升了演示采集的可靠性和效率，实证评估支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex通过嵌套式策略框架有效减轻了灵巧操作演示采集的负担，证明了利用学习到的手部技能辅助演示采集能够产生高质量演示，进而训练出有效的自主灵巧操作策略，为灵巧操作的学习与采集提供了一种可行的新范式。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [21] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIRE-VLA是一个失败感知的自进化框架，针对自动驾驶视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型在GRPO强化学习中的"相对奖励信号失效"问题。当采样轨迹质量差且多样性低时，模型从冻结的轮次起始副本中自蒸馏，教师与学生参数规模相同但教师能观察隐藏的未来轨迹，从而将未解决的失败转化为特权监督。实验表明在6,019个nuScenes样本上，G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500米，持续失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在每组轨迹中通过相对奖励差异进行学习，但当所有采样轨迹都较差时，相对信号只能对失败排序，无法识别失败区域之外的不良行为，导致政策陷入失败局部最优无法突破。

Method: 通过两个关键设计实现：1) 当奖励低且多样性低的组被触发时，从冻结的轮次起始同模型副本（教师）进行自蒸馏，教师能观察隐藏的未来轨迹而学生不能；2) 监督遵循学生的生成前缀并仅限制在答案token上，同时GRPO对所有组保持激活。更新后的策略成为下一轮的教师，使被路由的失败分布随策略演化而变化，无需更大的外部教师。

Result: 在6,019个150个保留nuScenes场景的样本上，与Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT相同起点对比（匹配学生rollout和策略更新次数）：单样本规划性能相当，G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500米，评估持续失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。误差降低主要来自罕见严重rollout的改善而非普通轨迹的均匀提升。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA通过失败信息自蒸馏有效解决了GRPO在"全失败"组中相对信号失效的问题，在不引入更大外部教师的情况下实现了更强的自动驾驶规划能力，且改进集中在高风险/严重失败场景上，表明该框架具有针对性的失败修正能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [22] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"驱动-感知协同设计"（actuation-perception co-design）理念的集成设计-建模-感知框架，通过在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件实现电缆张力实时测量，并基于多体短粗梁模型构建并行计算框架，结合近端多维力/力矩传感器实现绳驱连续体手术机器人的实时形状与力感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳驱连续体手术机器人在紧凑的绞盘组件内难以获取电缆张力信息，形状与力感知长期受限于此瓶颈，亟需在不牺牲紧凑绞盘架构的前提下实现集成感知能力。

Method: 1) 在驱动系统的电机安装支架中引入柔性元件，利用电缆反作用力引起的微变形实现实时张力测量；2) 提出基于多体短粗梁模型的并行计算框架，捕捉短梁段剪切效应及多电缆协同交互，同时满足实时性；3) 引入近端多维力/力矩传感器作为额外测量锚点，实现稳定的形状与力感知。

Result: 在单段和双段构型的连续体手术机器人上验证了该框架，实现了实时末端位姿估计以及接触力与接触位置的感知。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在不牺牲紧凑绞盘架构的前提下实现了电缆张力反馈，使绳驱连续体手术机器人具备集成感知能力，突破了形状与力感知的技术瓶颈。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [23] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Deliberate Practice (DP)的主动技能学习算法，在有限练习预算下通过双线性规划计算最优技能分配方案，使机器人获得最大化累积奖励，并在长期操控任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人技能学习通常面临有限练习预算的约束，而顺序任务中需要学习多种技能。如何在预算内选择练习哪些技能以最大化长期任务收益，是一个关键且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 将技能学习预算分配问题建模为双线性规划问题，综合考虑技能掌握所需时间与技能解锁任务计划的累积奖励，利用现成的求解器精确计算预算最优的技能分配方案。

Result: 在模拟环境和真实世界的长期操控任务实验中，该方法能够使机器人最优地利用有限的练习时间获取有用策略，并提升长期规划的效能。

Conclusion: DP算法为机器人技能学习提供了可证明预算最优的分配策略，有效解决了有限练习资源下顺序任务技能学习的组合优化难题。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [24] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Seeker提出了一种从动作中学习注意力的任务与状态条件化读出机制，无需外部空间标注即可生成与任务进度相关的感兴趣区域（ROI），用于RGB裁剪、背景增强和点云过滤，在仿真和真实机器人上将成功率从48.3%提升至76.7%，并显著增强了光照和背景变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉瓶颈方法依赖外部空间标签（如注视、物体类别、可供性标注）或动作派生裁剪（如以末端执行器为中心的固定裁剪）。动作派生裁剪虽然无需额外标签，但固定了事件时序、代理点和裁剪尺度，当控制所需的视觉证据远离末端执行器或随任务进度连续变化时，裁剪会错位，导致数据效率下降。

Method: Seeker从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，通过迭代更新查询向量聚合视觉证据，在纯动作监督下生成进度感知的ROI。该ROI作为空间接口用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强和点云过滤，实现"看哪里"与"如何行动"的解耦。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界中，Seeker在数据效率和鲁棒性上超越了无裁剪、增强和动作派生裁剪基线。真实机器人实验中，域内成功率从最佳基线的48.3%提升至76.7%，在光照/背景迁移下的成功率从20.0%提升至60.0%。

Conclusion: Seeker证明从动作监督中可以学习任务相关的空间注意力，避免了对人工标注的依赖，同时克服了动作派生裁剪的固定结构限制，实现了更灵活、鲁棒且样本高效的视觉运动学习。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [25] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactGuard是一个用于分块视觉运动策略的接触前执行监视器。它在策略规划的动作块被执⾏之前，预测其在潜在视觉空间中的短期后果，并在预测的后接触潜在表示可能指示失败时中止执行。其潜在世界模型从未标记的机器人轨迹中训练，仅在标记的预接触片段上训练轻量级失败探针，无需修改底层策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操控任务失败通常在机器人已经与物体接触后才被发现，尤其在腕部相机设置中，虽然近距离的夹爪-物体视图有助于观察接触，但不良的逼近动作可能在传统检测器反应之前就已经推挤、错过、滑动或扰动物体。作者希望构建一个能在接触发生之前预测失败的执行监视器，以提前中止策略执行。

Method: ContactGuard由两部分组成：(1) 潜在世界模型——从未标记的机器人轨迹中训练，用于预测在给定规划动作下紧凑的多视角视觉嵌入，避免像素级视频预测；(2) 轻量级失败探针——从少量标记的预接触片段中训练。部署时，ContactGuard在即将发生接触事件前锚定预测，在策略自身动作下滚动模型前向预测，并验证预测的后接触潜在表示是否指示失败。

Result: 在真实的接触丰富操控任务中，ContactGuard比直接消除和损坏动作消融基线更准确地预测失败，并能作为预接触中止信号传输到实时机器人，无需修改底层策略。

Conclusion: ContactGuard通过在接触前预测潜在视觉空间的未来结果来实现预接触失败检测，显著优于传统仅在接触后反应式检测的方法，且对底层策略透明，具有通用性和可部署性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [26] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架，用于社会机器人在领域增量式持续学习场景中学习社交恰当行为（如清洁、服务、开启对话）。EDD显式分离环境知识与社交代理相关知识，并利用基于回放的排练机制缓解灾难性遗忘，实验表明其在多个室内领域上优于现有最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会机器人需要在多样环境中运行，类似布置在不同场景下可能隐含不同的社交恰当行为（如在拥挤的家庭中开启对话可接受，但在办公室会议中则具干扰性）。由于无法预先穷举所有规范和环境，机器人需要持续学习能力来从序列经验中适应同时保留已学知识。然而，先前研究未曾涉及领域增量式持续学习场景，即在机器人逐步接触多元情境时，环境线索（如空间开放与否）和社交线索（如他人如何围绕机器人就位）需联合塑造机器人行为恰当性。

Method: 提出显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架：1）显式分离环境知识与社交代理相关知识为两个分支；2）使用基于回放的排练机制缓解持续学习中的遗忘问题；3）在多个室内领域（如客厅、会议室、办公室、走廊）上学习机器人行为恰当性（如清洁、服务、开启对话）。

Result: 实验表明EDD在多个领域增量式持续学习基准上优于若干最先进基线；消融研究进一步评估了不同解耦策略以及领域顺序敏感性。

Conclusion: EDD有效地填补了社会机器人在领域增量式持续学习场景中的空白，通过显式解耦环境与社交知识并辅以回放排练，能够在连续学习多样室内环境中社交恰当行为的同时缓解灾难性遗忘。代码已公开。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>


### [27] [Decoding Task Progress from VLA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13474)
*Atiksh Bhardwaj,Edward Weiyi Duan,Prithwish Dan,Wei-Chiu Ma,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用机械可解释性技术，对视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）π0.5的残差流进行探针分析，发现任务进度（轨迹归一化的剩余时间）可以线性地从激活中读取。该信号在预训练PaliGemma主干中就已存在，且单线性探针可泛化到未见任务，但无法用于策略引导。作者将该探针用作无标签OOD检测器，能有效检测停滞的任务进度，与SOTA方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型正在快速走向部署，但目前缺乏理解模型内部表征的基础工具和运行时监控手段。作者希望利用机械可解释性来揭示VLA内部的可读语义信号，并探索其在部署监控中的应用价值。

Method: 作者对π0.5的残差流进行线性探针（linear probing）分析，检验任务进度信号的可读性；在预训练PaliGemma主干上验证信号是否在机器人数据训练前已存在；通过多头任务训练检验跨任务泛化与语言反事实下的表现；并利用探针构建无标签OOD检测器用于检测停滞的任务进度。

Result: 任务进度信号在激活中线性可读；该信号在预训练PaliGemma主干中已存在（无需机器人数据）；单线性探针可泛化到未见任务，在多头提示（multi-prompt）训练下对语言反事实敏感，但无法有效引导策略；作为OOD检测器用于检测停滞进度时与SOTA方法竞争。

Conclusion: VLA模型内部包含丰富且线性可读的语义量表征（如任务进度），学习读取这些信号为监控部署中的视觉运动策略提供了一条轻量级、可解释的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [28] [A Browser-Native Digital Test Range for Benchmarking 4D Ocean-Glider Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13511)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个浏览器原生的数字试验场，用于对海洋滑翔机规划器进行可复现的重复在站评估。它通过统一的"规划-观测"契约，将手动航线、内置算法及外部（经典或学习型）规划器输出整合到同一评估框架中，并支持从区域选择到打分评估的完整实验流程。OSSE对照实验验证了5种经典规划器在54次任务中的表现，同时通过Pyodide/WebAssembly实现了GliderFlight的本地-浏览器一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋滑翔机规划器的重复在站评估需要稀缺的运载器、操作人员、布放与回收资源，且海洋条件无法为竞争性算法重置，导致评估成本高、可复现性差。需要一个无需安装、可控、可重置的数字测试环境来公平比较不同规划器。

Method: 提出了一个引导式、免安装的浏览器原生数字试验场。系统引导用户经历区域选择、任务范围化测深、时间/深度强迫、科学目标设定、可选任务分解、路线规划、洋流平流执行、观测生成与评分等全流程。核心创新是一个统一的"规划-观测"契约，统一了运载器、感知和评估器假设。利用Pyodide/WebAssembly实现GliderFlight 1.2.0的源码锁定与本地-浏览器一致性。

Result: 对照OSSE实验在2个episodes、3个确定性种子、校准的60小时时域上评估了5种经典规划器。所有54次任务均无硬违规完成并回收，规划器排名与潜水策略效应揭示了运行-科学权衡。真实公开部署提供了场参考审计以划定当前运动学边界。

Conclusion: 该数字试验场为任务规模部署前实验提供了科学可追溯、组件合格化的运行空间，并建立了高保真多层级仿真的路径。通过统一的契约与浏览器原生架构，解决了海洋规划器评估的可复现性与资源约束问题。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [29] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanTracker基准与HumanScore评估指标，用于人形机器人运动跟踪评测。HumanTracker包含约153小时多专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，分为四个运动类别并附带文本标签。HumanScore是在1.2万对（2.4万个）运动样本上训练的对齐人类偏好的指标。实验表明HumanScore能更好预测人类偏好，并揭示传统运动学指标常忽略的接触与稳定性故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形运动跟踪评估依赖平均逐帧位姿差异（运动学误差），但这与人类在视频中感知到的质量不一致，尤其忽略了不稳定支撑和错误接触（如脚滑、触地时机错误）等关键物理伪影。此外，现有测试集规模小且多样性不足，难以覆盖接触密集和长时程行为。

Method: 提出HumanTracker基准集（约153小时多表演者光学运动轨迹，4个运动类别，含文本标签支持细粒度诊断）；提出HumanScore指标，在12K运动对（含24K运动）上训练，使其与人类偏好对齐。

Result: 在多个代表性SOTA跟踪器上的评估表明，HumanScore比运动学指标更准确地预测人类偏好，并能捕获运动学指标常遗漏的接触失败与稳定性失效问题。

Conclusion: HumanTracker与HumanScore将人形运动跟踪评估从仅看逐帧位姿误差转向感知对齐的多维度评估，具备可扩展性，为接触密集和长时程运动跟踪提供了更可靠的评测工具。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 模型在威斯康星州南部来自EAGLE-I平台的多样化停电事件上得到验证；与朴素梯形AUC确定性估计的直接比较表明，后验均值能复现相同的点估计，同时提供确定性方法结构上无法给出的校准的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 该框架为公用事业和应急规划人员提供了在不确定性下基准化恢复动态、比较停电事件的规范工具，兼具点估计准确性与完整不确定性量化的优势。

Abstract: We propose a hierarchical Bayesian model for estimating time-varying outage risk from county-level power outage data. The model combines cubic B-spline basis functions with a Beta-Binomial likelihood to capture smooth, nonlinear recovery trajectories while accommodating overdispersion in observed customer counts. A shared hyperprior on the Beta-Binomial concentration parameter enables hierarchical shrinkage across geographically indexed groups, allowing sparse or short-lived events to borrow statistical strength from the broader population. Posterior inference is conducted via the No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS) in PyMC, yielding full posterior distributions over latent outage probabilities and derived resilience metrics including the area under the risk curve (AUC). We assess predictive performance using posterior predictive coverage, RMSE, and leave-one-out cross-validation, and demonstrate the model across a heterogeneous set of outage events in southern Wisconsin drawn from the EAGLE-I power outage monitoring platform. A direct comparison against naive trapezoidal AUC estimation confirms that the posterior mean recovers the same point estimates as deterministic integration while providing calibrated uncertainty quantification that deterministic approaches structurally cannot. The framework offers utilities and emergency planners a principled tool for benchmarking recovery dynamics and comparing outage events under uncertainty.

</details>

Method: 以维多利亚州2017-2025年为案例，首先评估AEF嵌入能否重建传统野火易发性分析中常用的物理变量；随后基于卫星获取的火灾发生数据，训练嵌入驱动的野火易发性模型，评估其ROC-AUC表现和空间识别能力；最后通过将维多利亚训练的模型迁移至堪培拉和西悉尼-蓝山地区，检验AEF嵌入的气候相似近邻区域迁移性。

Conclusion: AEF嵌入在野火易发性制图中具有显著价值，既可用于重建传统物理变量降低数据预处理负担，又具备良好的区域间可迁移性，为政府机构和（再）保险公司等下游用户提供了可扩展的野火易发性制图工作流程基础。

Abstract: Wildfire susceptibility mapping typically relies on physical variables assembled from multiple remote-sensing, climate, and geospatial products. AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) provides analysis-ready geospatial embeddings that may reduce this dependence on heavy harmonisation and task-specific feature engineering, but their value for wildfire susceptibility mapping has not been systematically evaluated. Using Victoria, Australia (2017-2025), as a case study, we show that AEF embeddings can reconstruct commonly used variables in wildfire susceptibility analysis with high accuracy. In downstream susceptibility models trained on satellite-derived fire occurrence data, embedding-based susceptibility models achieve ROC-AUC values above 0.92 and consistently identify high wildfire susceptibility across eastern Victoria, particularly Gippsland and the north-eastern uplands, with additional localized hotspots in central and northwestern Victoria. A key feature of AEF embeddings is their strong near-region transferability within climatically similar regions. When embedding-based models trained in Victoria are applied to Canberra and Western Sydney-Blue Mountains, ROC-AUC improves by around 4% at Canberra and declines by around 2% at Western Sydney-Blue Mountains, compared with a mean decrease of approximately 25% for physical-variable models. These findings provide practical guidance for using AEF embeddings and lay a foundation for scalable wildfire susceptibility mapping workflows for downstream users such as government agencies and (re)insurers.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向生物标志物测量与生存结局的联合分析中，两步法和贝叶斯联合模型是两种常用方法，但鲜有研究在真实临床数据上系统比较二者的推断结果差异及其原因。作者希望通过实际HIV临床试验数据阐明不同方法对纵向信息处理方式的差异如何影响最终统计推断。

Method: 采用两步法和贝叶斯联合模型两种方法分析HIV临床试验数据（467名患者，重复CD4测量，全因死亡为终点）。两步法先拟合线性混合效应模型，将每名患者预测的基线CD4值作为固定协变量纳入Cox比例风险模型；联合模型则同时建模纵向CD4过程和生存过程，将死亡风险与当前潜在CD4值关联。

Result: 两种方法估计的ddI与死亡关联方向与幅度相似（两步法HR=1.342, 95%CI: 1.006-1.789；联合模型HR=1.383, 95%CrI: 0.952-2.010）；CD4的保护性关联在联合模型下更强（HR=0.776 vs 0.826）。由于两种方法使用了不同的纵向CD4过程摘要，差异不能仅归因于测量误差或信息性脱落。

Conclusion: 纵向生物标志物信息的处理方式（使用基线预测值还是当前潜在值）会对统计推断产生实质性影响，研究者应根据研究问题和纵向过程的特点谨慎选择分析方法。

Abstract: The two-stage approach and Bayesian joint modeling are commonly used to analyze longitudinal biomarker measurements together with time-to-event outcomes. Using data from an HIV clinical trial of 467 patients with repeated CD4 measurements and all-cause mortality as the survival outcome, we compared a baseline two-stage approach with a Bayesian joint model. The two-stage analysis fitted a linear mixed-effects model and included each patient's predicted baseline CD4 value as a fixed covariate in a Cox proportional hazards model. The joint model simultaneously modeled the longitudinal CD4 process and survival while linking mortality risk to the current underlying CD4 value. The estimated association between ddI and mortality was similar in direction and magnitude across the two approaches. The two-stage estimate was HR = 1.342 (95% CI: 1.006-1.789), whereas the joint-model estimate was HR = 1.383 (95% CrI: 0.952-2.010). The estimated protective association of CD4 was stronger under the joint model (HR = 0.776) than under the two-stage approach (HR = 0.826). Because the approaches used different summaries of the longitudinal CD4 process, the observed differences cannot be attributed solely to measurement error or informative dropout. The findings demonstrate that the treatment of longitudinal biomarker information can materially affect statistical inference.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: （1）将面数据嵌入规则网格；（2）提取局部窗口；（3）利用离散小波变换获得多分辨率表示，将每个窗口表示为尺度与方向特定的细节系数；（4）定义基于这些系数的不相似度度量，并在旋转和反射下对其进行最小化；（5）识别具有相似空间结构的连续统计单元集合。

Abstract: Socioeconomic indicators in England exhibit complex spatial patterns that are not well captured by standard approaches based on averages or broad geographic classifications. We propose a method for comparing areas based on their internal spatial structure, using a multiresolution representation derived from the discrete wavelet transform. The method embeds areal data into a regular grid, extracts local windows, and represents each as a set of scale- and direction-specific detail coefficients. A dissimilarity measure, defined over these coefficients and minimised over rotations and reflections, is used to identify contiguous sets of statistical units with similar spatial structure. We apply the approach to England's 2025 Index of Multiple Deprivation at the lower layer super output area level. We show that areas with similar internal structure are often found across regions, levels of urbanisation, and average deprivation, challenging the use of these categories as proxies for local geography. The results provide a framework for identifying comparable places based on how deprivation is distributed within them, with implications for policy evaluation and transferability.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息卷积自编码器，直接从电化学阻抗谱（EIS）估计弛豫时间分布（DRT），无需频谱特定调参。网络在训练中嵌入阻抗与DRT的离散关系，约束输出与阻抗一致。在合成双ZARC谱上能解析重叠弛豫过程，并在三个独立固体氧化物燃料电池/电解池数据集上实现低于1.1%的归一化误差。解码器探针分析表明潜在表征按弛豫时间尺度组织，潜在空间距离可捕捉运行变化、氢短缺事件和长期退化。同一轻量架构无需修改即可跨数据集应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从EIS估计DRT是一个病态逆问题，对正则化选择高度敏感，现有方法需要针对每条频谱进行特定调参，缺乏通用性和鲁棒性。

Method: 采用物理信息卷积自编码器：将阻抗与DRT之间的离散化解析关系嵌入训练过程作为约束，使网络输出阻抗一致的DRT分布；通过解码器探针分析揭示潜在空间的组织结构。

Result: 在合成双ZARC谱上成功分辨重叠弛豫过程；在三个独立SOFC/SOEC数据集上归一化误差低于1.1%；潜在空间距离能够反映运行条件变化、氢短缺事件和长期退化。

Abstract: Estimating the distribution of relaxation times (DRT) fromelectrochemical impedance spectroscopy (EIS) is an ill-posed inverse problem that is highly sensitive to regularisation choices. We propose a physics-informed convolutional autoencoder that estimates DRT directly from EIS data without spectrum-specific tuning. A discretised relation between impedance and the DRT is embedded in the training process, constraining the network to produce impedance-consistent distributions. The model resolves overlapping relaxation processes in synthetic two-ZARC spectra and accurately reconstructs measurements from three independent solid oxide fuel and electrolysis cell datasets, with range-normalised errors below 1.1%. Decoder-probe analysis shows that the learned latent representation is organised according to relaxation timescale. Distances in this latent space capture operating changes, hydrogen-shortage events, and long-term degradation. The same lightweight architecture is applied across all datasets without modification, providing consistent DRT estimation and an interpretable basis for condition monitoring.

</details>


### [35] [A Metric Space of Spatial Graphs: Two-Sample Testing, Data Depth, and Application to Cardiac Fibrosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13406)
*Anna Calissano,Arstanbek Okenov,Katja Zeppenfeld,Alexander Panfilov*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于空间图（spatial graph）的心脏纤维化组织分析方法：通过骨架化将纤维化斑块表示为空间图，引入具有旋转不变性的Fused Gromov Wasserstein度量的空间图空间，实现分布级统计检验、深度度量以及新型可视化工具DepthPlot，从而从几何和拓扑角度揭示心脏纤维化纹理的患者特异性特征及临床意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏纤维化会降低电传导性能并引发心律失常，而心律失常波通常围绕非导电的纤维化斑块旋转，因此斑块的几何和拓扑结构对心律失常动力学至关重要。尽管这些结构具有重要的临床相关性，但目前对其理解仍然不足，缺乏有效的定量分析框架来比较和表征不同患者心脏中纤维化斑块的空间结构。

Method: 1) 通过骨架化（skeletonization）将每个纤维化斑块表示为空间图，节点嵌入欧氏空间，边编码底层组织的几何属性；2) 引入空间图空间（spatial graph space），配备旋转不变的Fused Gromov Wasserstein（FGW）度量，支持比较节点和边数量不同的空间图；3) 基于此度量进行分布级统计检验和深度度量分析；4) 提出DepthPlot——一种用于度量空间中深度度量的新型可视化工具。

Result: 将方法应用于人心脏组织病理图像的比较分析，发现纤维化纹理表现出强烈的患者特异性特征，同时部分心脏显示出显著的几何相似性，可能反映共享的病理突变或其他未知因素。通过定量深度度量，研究表征了中心和外周空间图，证明了所提出的框架能够产生统计上和临床上均有意义的纤维化纹理表征结果。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将空间图与FGW度量相结合，为心脏纤维化斑块的几何拓扑结构提供了系统性量化框架，验证了其在统计检验和深度分析方面的有效性，并为理解纤维化与心律失常的关系提供了新的临床洞察工具。

Abstract: Cardiac fibrosis reduces electrical conductivity and is a leading cause of arrhythmia. Arrhythmic waves typically rotate around non-conducting fibrotic patches, so the geometry and topology of these patches (spatially isolated regions of fibrotic tissue within the heart muscle) play an important role in arrhythmia dynamics. Despite their clinical relevance, these structures remain poorly understood. We address this open problem using histopathological images of human hearts affected by cardiac fibrosis. Each patch is represented as a spatial graph via skeletonization, where nodes are embedded as points in Euclidean space and edges encode geometric properties of the underlying tissue. The core methodological contribution of this work is the introduction of spatial graph space, a metric space equipped with a rotation-invariant Fused Gromov Wasserstein metric that enables comparison of spatial graphs with differing numbers of nodes and edges. Building on this, we perform a distribution-level statistical testing and depth measures for spatial graphs. To enable the interpretation of the spatial graph sample distribution, we introduce DepthPlot, a novel visualization tool for depth measures in metric spaces. Applying our methodology to compare patients and the spatial position of patches within the ventricles, we find that fibrotic textures exhibit strong patient-specific features, while some hearts display notable geometric similarities, potentially reflecting shared pathological mutations or other unknown factors. Through quantitative depth measures, we characterize test outcomes via central and peripheral spatial graphs, demonstrating that the proposed framework yields statistically and clinically meaningful insights into fibrotic texture characterization.

</details>
