TL;DR: 本文报道了Eu₂MnSi₂O₇这一黄长石型绝缘亚铁磁体中存在平均场临界行为。通过磁化测量和中子粉末衍射实验，发现其临界指数接近平均场值，表明长程偶极相互作用支配了该绝缘亚铁磁体的渐近临界行为。磁结构由磁空间群P2₁2₁′2′描述，呈现由Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用驱动的倾斜亚铁磁构型。这是首次在绝缘亚铁磁体中观察到偶极相互作用驱动平均场临界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索平均场理论在复杂绝缘磁体中的适用性，特别是寻找由长程偶极相互作用而非短程交换相互作用支配临界行为的绝缘亚铁磁体材料平台。

Method: 结合磁化强度测量和中子粉末衍射技术，测定临界指数、精修磁结构，并通过磁空间群分析确定自旋构型及相互作用机制。

Result: 临界指数接近平均场值；磁结构属于P2₁2₁′2′磁空间群，呈现由DM相互作用驱动的倾斜亚铁磁构型；这是首个由偶极相互作用驱动平均场临界性的绝缘亚铁磁体。

Conclusion: 长程偶极相互作用在该材料中主导渐近临界行为，将平均场理论的应用范围扩展到复杂绝缘磁体，并为探索亚铁磁性与长程相互作用提供了新的研究平台。

Abstract: We report mean-field critical behavior in Eu$_2$MnSi$_2$O$_7$, a melilite-type ferrimagnet with spin-only Eu$^{2+}$ and Mn$^{2+}$ moments and negligible orbital contributions. Magnetization measurements combined with neutron powder diffraction reveal critical exponents close to the mean-field values, indicating that long-range dipolar interactions govern the asymptotic critical behavior in this insulating ferrimagnet. The refined magnetic structure, described by the magnetic space group $P2_12_1^\prime2^\prime$, exhibits a tilted ferrimagnetic configuration driven by the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, reflecting the noncentrosymmetric nature of the lattice. These results extend the applicability of mean-field theory to complex insulating magnets and establish Eu$_2$MnSi$_2$O$_7$ as a platform for exploring ferrimagnetism and long-range interactions. To our knowledge, this is the first insulating ferrimagnet in which dipolar interactions drive mean-field criticality.

</details>


### [102] [Correlation versus Causation in Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12770)
*Conrad Wichmann,Ryan Thorngren,Ruben Verresen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了"静态磁化率/因果关联"这一新概念——即通过微扰哈密顿量来测量关联函数变化，并发现其衰减比传统关联函数快最多15个数量级。作者将该机制归因于时间导数场无法贡献静态响应，并在高维CFT中证明领头因果关联来自primary场，利用DMRG识别出(2+1)D临界Ising模型中两个先前未分辨的primary场。该机制还统一解释了(1+1)D无能隙SPT相的边模局域化反常小分裂，并构造了分裂小至1/L^18和1/L^25的自旋链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关联函数的空间衰减仅反映缩放维度Δ，作者希望引入一种新的"因果"测量（静态磁化率）来揭示更多物理信息。他们发现这种静态响应比关联函数衰减更快，可以揭示此前难以分辨的primary场，并理解边缘模局域化的反常行为。

Method: 理论分析CFT中静态磁化率与关联函数的对比，指出时间导数场不能贡献静态响应的通用机制；在高维CFT中证明leading causation来自primary而非descendant；利用DMRG数值计算；构造(1+1)D gapless SPT相自旋链模型。

Result: 因果关联的衰减可比关联函数快多达15个数量级；在(2+1)D临界Ising模型中识别出Δ≈8.8的角落primary和Δ≈4.6的重磁线缺陷primary；构造了边缘模分裂小至1/L^18和1/L^25的自旋链。

Conclusion: 静态磁化率（因果关联）是揭示CFT中隐藏结构的强大工具，其通用机制不仅适用于CFT，还统一解释了(1+1)D gapless SPT相中的边模局域化，为量子模拟和数值研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Correlation functions $\langle O_1(x) O_2(0) \rangle$ reveal scaling dimensions through spatial decay. We instead consider static susceptibility, the change in $\langle O_1(x) \rangle$ from perturbing the Hamiltonian by $O_2(0)$, which we term causation for short. In a conformal field theory (CFT), dimensional analysis predicts decay of $|x|^{-2Δ}$ for correlation and $|x|^{-2Δ+1}$ for causation. Yet we find causation can decay up to fifteen additional orders in $x$ through a general mechanism, which we trace to time-derivative fields being unable to contribute to static response. In higher-dimensional CFTs, this mechanism ensures leading causation arises from primaries, even when descendants dominate correlation, which we leverage with DMRG to identify a previously unresolved corner primary of $Δ\approx 8.8$ and a heavy magnetic line defect primary of $Δ\approx 4.6$ in the $(2+1)$D critical Ising model. Moreover, the same mechanism governs edge-mode localization in $(1+1)$D gapless symmetry-protected topological phases, explaining previously observed anomalously small edge-mode splittings and guiding our construction of spin chains with splittings as small as $1/L^{18}$ and $1/L^{25}$.

</details>


### [103] [Topology and Quantum-Spin-Classical-Spin Crossover of the Gapped Kondo Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13065)
*David Krüger,Michael Potthoff*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过Lanczos变换和自洽组态相互作用方法，系统地研究了几种带隙Kondo效应（gapped Kondo effect）的局部相图，并利用多种拓扑不变量（动量空间陈数、B空间陈数、S空间陈数）对不同相进行了表征，同时探讨了从量子杂质自旋到经典杂质自旋的连续过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带隙Kondo效应描述了有限硬带隙的导电子系统中局域杂质自旋的屏蔽现象，但其相图结构和拓扑性质尚不清楚。特别是，量子杂质自旋与经典极限（B→∞或S→∞）之间的相图是否连续连接，以及不同Kondo实现（欠屏蔽、过屏蔽）的拓扑表征，都是需要深入研究的问题。

Method: 本文采用Lanczos变换与自洽组态相互作用（CI）方案相结合的方法进行数值研究。通过引入三种拓扑不变量来表征相：常规动量空间陈数（对应Chern绝缘体）、B空间陈数（在B→0极限下定义，耦合杂质自旋与虚构局域磁场）、以及S空间陈数（定义在固定长度的经典杂质自旋上）。通过调节磁场强度B，可以在量子杂质自旋与经典杂质自旋哈密顿量之间连续插值。对于欠屏蔽Kondo效应，通过B→∞或S→∞趋近经典自旋极限，并运用不同类型的散射理论解释所得相。对于双通道过屏蔽Kondo效应，则研究了自发粒子-空穴对称性破缺现象。

Result: 研究表明，通过调节磁场强度，量子杂质自旋与经典杂质自旋哈密顿量之间的相图存在连续性。欠屏蔽Kondo效应在经典自旋极限下与量子情况的行为可通过散射理论加以解释。双通道过屏蔽Kondo效应表现出自发粒子-空穴对称性破缺，从而产生了高度非平凡的量子-经典相图。

Conclusion: 带隙Kondo效应在不同自旋场景（S=1/2量子自旋、S>1/2欠屏蔽、双通道过屏蔽）下展现丰富且不同的相图结构，可通过多种拓扑不变量加以表征，且量子与经典自旋极限之间存在连续的物理联系。粒子-空穴对称性破缺在过屏蔽情况下扮演关键角色。

Abstract: The gapped Kondo effect describes the screening of an $S=\frac12$ impurity spin locally coupled via an antiferromagnetic exchange interaction to a conduction-electron system exhibiting a finite hard gap. Using a combination of a Lanczos transformation and a self-consistent configuration-interaction scheme, we numerically investigate the local phase diagram. Furthermore, we show that the different phases can be characterized by several topological invariants: the conventional momentum-space Chern number of the underlying two-dimensional host system, corresponding to a Chern insulator; the B-space Chern number, defined by coupling the impurity spin to a fictitious local magnetic field $\boldsymbol B$, in the limit $B \to 0$; and the S-space Chern number, defined for a classical impurity spin, i.e., a vector of fixed length. The classical-spin limit is obtained for $B \to \infty$. By varying the field strength, we can therefore continuously interpolate between quantum-impurity-spin and classical-impurity-spin Hamiltonians and investigate whether the corresponding phase diagrams are likewise continuously connected. The gapped underscreened Kondo effect is studied for an impurity spin $S>\frac12$ as well as in the classical-spin limit approached via $B \to \infty$ or $S \to \infty$. Different variants of scattering theory are employed to interpret the resulting phases. Finally, the gapped two-channel overscreened Kondo effect, realized by coupling a quantum impurity spin equally to the local electron spins of both orbitals within a unit cell, is shown to be characterized by spontaneous particle-hole symmetry breaking. This leads to a highly nontrivial quantum-classical phase diagram.

</details>


### [104] [Magnetism in antiperovskite (Li$_2$\textit{M})\textit{Ch}O (\textit{M} = Fe, Mn, Co; \textit{Ch} = S, Se) diluted magnets with fixed 1/3 filling: the key role of magnetic anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13111)
*Jieyuan Zheng,Frederik L. Carstens,Lennart Singer,Mohammed A. A. Mohamed,Luca Bischof,Alexey Alfonsov,Jan Arneth,Silke Hampel,Nico Gräßler,Rüdiger Klingeler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了系列锂富集反钙钛矿材料(Li₂M)ChO（M=Fe, Co, Mn; Ch=Se, S）的磁性质。由于过渡金属和锂离子在X位随机分布，形成强稀释的磁子晶格（1/3填充，略高于渗流阈值）。研究发现缺乏明显的居里-外斯行为，但存在长程反铁磁有序，Néel温度随过渡金属从Mn（~30 K）到Fe（~50 K）到Co（70-90 K）递增。关键调控参数是过渡金属的磁各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定1/3填充（接近但略高于渗流阈值）的强稀释磁子晶格中，系统研究自旋大小、磁各向异性和轨道构型对磁有序演化的影响，揭示稀释体系中的磁耦合机制和关键调控因素。

Method: 对系列反钙钛矿材料(Li₂M)ChO（M=Fe, Co, Mn; Ch=Se, S）进行系统的磁性质测量，包括磁化率随温度的变化、电子自旋共振（ESR）等实验手段，并比较不同过渡金属和硫族元素对磁有序温度的影响。

Result: 1) 在350 K以上未观察到明显的居里-外斯行为，磁化率较大且弱依赖于温度；2) 观察到明确的长程反铁磁有序，Néel温度从(Li₂Mn)ChO的~30 K到(Li₂Fe)ChO的~50 K再到(Li₂Co)ChO的70-90 K；3) 除Co外，硫族元素对T_N影响不大；4) 室温下存在宽的ESR信号，表明T_N以上存在显著磁耦合和短程磁关联；5) 结构参数（容忍因子、键角）对T_N影响不大，磁各向异性是关键参数。

Conclusion: 在该1/3填充的强稀释磁子晶格体系中，磁有序温度被稀释显著降低，但磁耦合和短程磁关联在远超T_N的温度仍存在。过渡金属的磁各向异性是决定Néel温度的关键因素，而结构参数的影响相对次要。

Abstract: We report the magnetic properties of a series of lithium-rich antiperovskites (Li$_2M$)$Ch$O ($M$ = Fe, Co, Mn and $Ch$ = Se, S) where transition metal and lithium ions are randomly distributed on the X-sites of the X$_3$BA structure, thereby forming a strongly diluted magnetic sublattice. Our study hence enables us to investigate the evolution of magnetic order at fixed 1/3-filling -- which is in the vicinity but slightly above the percolation threshold -- upon variation of the spin size, the magnetic anisotropy, and the orbital configuration. The data imply the absence of a distinct Curie-Weiss behavior up to 350~K but show rather large and weakly temperature-dependent magnetic susceptibility. We observe clear signatures of long-range antiferromagnetic order evolving in the 1/3-filled and strongly diluted magnetic X-site lattice with increasing Néel temperatures from $T_{\rm{N}}\simeq 30$~K in (Li$_2$Mn)$Ch$O to $\simeq 50$~K in (Li$_2$Fe)$Ch$O and $70-90$~K in (Li$_2$Co)$Ch$O. Except for $M$ = Co, the chalcogenide has no sizable effect on $T_{\rm N}$. We conclude significant magnetic coupling and short-range magnetic correlations at well above $T_{\rm N}$ which is in line with the observation of a broad electron spin resonance signal at room temperature. The actual ordering temperatures are strongly diminished by magnetic dilution. While structural parameters such as the tolerance factor and bonding angles do not strongly affect $T_{\rm N}$, a key parameter is the magnetic anisotropy of the transition metals.

</details>


### [105] [Accelerating a Strong-Coupling Non-Equilibrium Steady-State Impurity Solver using (Quantics) Tensor Trains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13146)
*Bastian Schindler,Martin Eckstein*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并比较了四种不同的积分元参数化方案，利用张量交叉插值获得低秩（quantics）张量训练表示，以高效计算强耦合展开中的高阶图解修正项。通过高斯基准验证精度和计算复杂度，并在自洽平衡及非平衡DMFT计算中验证至三阶，同时扩展到具有延迟密度-密度相互作用的杂质模型和非平衡扩展DMFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合展开中高阶图解修正项的计算主要受限于高维、含时有序积分的求值，亟需高效的数值方法来降低计算成本。

Method: 比较四种不同的积分元参数化方式以获得低秩quantics张量训练表示，采用张量交叉插值技术；特别强调quantics时间差分解中直接以quantics张量训练形式执行延迟卷积；使用高斯受控基准分析精度、键维度和计算扩展性，并应用于自洽DMFT及扩展DMFT计算。

Result: 张量交叉插值显著降低了高阶图解的计算成本；验证了计算可扩展至强耦合展开的三阶；成功将求解器扩展到具有延迟密度-密度相互作用的杂质模型，并应用于非平衡扩展DMFT。

Conclusion: 张量交叉插值提供了一种受控且可系统改进的框架，用于非平衡量子杂质计算，有效解决了高维时有序积分带来的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Including higher order diagrammatic corrections to the strong-coupling expansion is mainly limited by the evaluation of high-dimensional, time-ordered integrals. In this work we present and compare four different parametrizations of the integrands in order to obtain a low-rank (quantics) tensor-train representation using tensor cross interpolation. Particular emphasis is placed on a quantics time-difference formulation in which the required retarded convolutions are performed directly in quantics tensor-train form. Using controlled Gaussian benchmarks, we analyze the accuracy, bond dimensions, and computational scaling of the different approaches. We then validate the most promising formulations in self-consistent equilibrium and nonequilibrium DMFT calculations and demonstrate calculations up to the third order in the strong-coupling expansion. Finally, we extend the solver to impurity models with retarded density-density interactions and apply it within nonequilibrium extended DMFT. Our results show that tensor cross interpolation substantially reduces the cost of evaluating higher-order diagrams and provides a controlled, systematically improvable framework for nonequilibrium quantum impurity calculations.

</details>


### [106] [Emergent Symmetry-Protected Topological Phases via Polyakov Confinement in Quantum Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13238)
*Li-Wei He,Shun-Li Yu,Jian-Xin Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明了Polyakov禁闭机制可以作为动力学引擎，将Dirac自旋液体转变为对称性保护拓扑（SPT）态。通过大尺度变分蒙特卡洛模拟，在具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用的三角晶格反铁磁体中证实了这一禁闭SPT相，其特征包括Wilson圈的面积律和消失的拓扑纠缠熵，并识别出可直接编码Dirac锥Berry曲率的自旋泵响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子自旋系统中涌现的分数化激发和规范结构如何重新禁闭形成新的物相，是一个基础性的开放挑战。作者希望探索一条将规范场论中的禁闭机制与拓扑物态联系起来的全新路径。

Method: 从具有自旋依赖规范通量的Dirac自旋液体出发，利用物理希尔伯特空间中固有的单占据约束触发磁单极子凝聚，动态禁闭体自旋子同时保持无能隙边缘模。采用大尺度变分蒙特卡洛模拟，在含DM相互作用的三角晶格反铁磁体上进行数值验证。

Result: 提供了该禁闭SPT相的微观证据：Wilson圈呈现特征性的面积律，拓扑纠缠熵消失。同时识别出自旋泵响应的可测量信号，该响应直接编码了母体Dirac锥的Berry曲率。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一条此前未被探索的通往SPT物理的路径，将规范场论、量子磁性与拓扑物质三个领域联系起来。Polyakov禁闭机制可作为自旋子版本的量子自旋霍尔效应的动力学引擎。

Abstract: A main theme in modern condensed matter physics is the emergence of fractionalized excitations and gauge structures from quantum spin systems. However, understanding how these exotic degrees of freedom reconfine into new phases of matter remains a fundamental challenge. In this work, we demonstrate that Polyakov's confinement mechanism-a foundation of compact gauge theory-can serve as a dynamical engine to transform a Dirac spin liquid into a symmetry-protected topological (SPT) state. Starting with a Dirac spin liquid with spin-dependent gauge fluxes, we show that the single-occupancy constraint inherent to the physical Hilbert space triggers monopole condensation, dynamically confining bulk spinons while preserving gapless edge modes-realizing a spinon analog of the quantum spin Hall effect. Using a large-scale variational Monte Carlo simulation on a triangular antiferromagnet with an additional Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, we provide microscopic evidence for this confined SPT phase, including a characteristic area law for the Wilson loop and vanishing topological entanglement entropy. Furthermore, we identify a measurable spin-pump response under magnetic fields, which directly encodes the Berry curvature of the parent Dirac cones. Our results reveal a previously unexplored pathway to SPT physics, bridging the fields of gauge theory, quantum magnetism, and topological matter.

</details>


### [107] [Imaginary-time correlations in time-sliced stochastic series expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13477)
*Ryan Flynn,Adam Iaizzi,Sibin Yang,Ying-Jer Kao,Anders W. Sandvik*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在随机级数展开（SSE）框架内高效且通用的算法，用于计算对角和非对角算子的虚时关联函数，通过对SSE算子串进行离散虚时间切片实现，该方法在横向场Ising模型和XXZ自旋链上验证了与精确对角化的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法与数值解析延拓技术结合可以从虚时关联函数中提取实频动力学性质，但虚时关联函数的有效计算在某些模型中具有挑战性，特别是当算子在对角化基下是非对角时。现有的方法在处理非对角算子时效率低下或存在困难，因此需要一种通用且高效的算法来评估各种算子的虚时关联函数。

Method: 该算法基于对SSE算子串进行离散虚时间切片，在明确定义的虚时时间网格点上提供无离散化误差的关联函数。对于非对角算子，作者推导了可直接集成到现有SSE有向环或簇更新方案中的估计量，仅引入极小的计算开销。

Result: 在一维横向场Ising模型（使用簇更新采样）和XXZ自旋链（使用有向环采样）上进行了基准测试，与小型系统的精确对角化结果表现出极好的一致性（仅存在统计误差）。此外，对更大系统的研究证明了该方法的效率。

Conclusion: 该方法为QMC计算虚时关联函数提供了一种通用且高效的解决方案，适用于对角和非对角算子，能够无缝集成到现有的SSE更新方案中，为实频动力学性质的提取奠定了高效基础。

Abstract: Combined with numerical analytic continuation techniques, quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) methods enable the extraction of real-frequency dynamical properties from imaginary-time correlation functions. However, the efficient computation of imaginary-time correlation functions by QMC simulations can (depending on the particular model used) be challenging, particularly for operators that are off-diagonal in the computational basis. In this work, we present an efficient and general algorithm within the stochastic series expansion (SSE) framework for evaluating imaginary-time correlation functions of both diagonal and off-diagonal operators. The algorithm builds on a discrete imaginary-time slicing of the SSE operator string, which provides correlation functions on a grid of well-defined imaginary-time points with no discretization error. For off-diagonal operators, we derive estimators that integrate directly into the existing SSE directed-loop or cluster updating schemes, introducing only minimal computational overhead. We benchmark the method on the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model (sampling with cluster updates) and XXZ spin chain (using directed-loop sampling), demonstrating excellent agreement (with only statistical errors) with exact diagonalization of small systems. We also study larger systems to demonstrate efficiency.

</details>

### [108] [On Waring--Goldbach problem with mixed powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12397)
*Huimin Wang,Shuangrui Tian*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了每一个充分大的奇数n都可以表示为一个素数的平方、立方、四次方、五次方、六次方和两个素数的八次方之和，即 p1²+p2²+p3³+p4⁴+p5⁵+p6⁶+p7⁸，这是对Cai和Mu（2015）工作的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于改进Cai和Mu于2015年已有的结果，将奇数在素数幂次和形式下的表示范围进一步扩展，即在原有的结果基础上引入更高次幂（八次方），并证明该方程对充分大的奇数可解。

Method: 本文采用解析数论中的Hardy-Littlewood圆法（circle method）来证明该素数方程的可解性，通过精细处理指数和的估计与奇异级数的分析，对充分大的奇数n建立素数幂次和的表示。

Result: 证明了对于所有充分大的奇数n，方程 n = p1²+p2²+p3³+p4⁴+p5⁵+p6⁶+p7⁸ 在素数中有解，这一结果改进了Cai和Mu在2015年的工作。

Conclusion: 该结果将奇数可表示为素数幂次和的形式进一步推广到包含八次方项的情况，推进了Waring-Goldbach型问题的研究进展。

Abstract: It is proved that for every sufficiently large odd integer $n$, the equation $$ n=p_1^2+p_2^2+p_3^3+p_4^4+p_5^5+p_6^6+p_7^8 $$ is solvable in primes. This refines the work of Cai and Mu in 2015.

</details>


### [109] [Generalized Addition Theorems for the Jacobi Elliptic Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12399)
*Efe Gürel*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了Jacobi椭圆函数的经典加法定理，给出了多个隐式和显式公式，包括涉及多个Jacobi函数的行列式恒等式、单个函数的显式加法定理，以及4、5、6、7项乃至一般的n项加法公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Jacobi椭圆函数的加法定理是数学物理中的重要工具，但现有的经典加法定理形式有限。本文旨在推广这些加法定理，提供更一般、更多项数的加法公式，以丰富和拓展该领域的研究。

Method: 本文首先推导涉及多个Jacobi函数的行列式恒等式，在此基础上证明单个Jacobi函数的显式加法公式，进而构造并验证4项、5项、6项、7项乃至一般的n项加法公式。

Result: 得到了多种新的隐式和显式加法公式，包括行列式恒等式、单函数的显式公式，以及4至7项和一般n项加法公式。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地推广了Jacobi椭圆函数的加法定理，提供了从低阶到一般n项的统一框架，为后续在数学物理中的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we generalize the classical addition theorems for the Jacobi elliptic functions, giving numerous implicit and explicit formulas. We first present determinant identities involving several of the Jacobi functions. Then, we prove explicit addition formulas for a single function. Novel 4-,5-,6- and 7-term addition formulas are also given alongside a general $n$-term addition formula.

</details>


### [110] [A conditional arithmetic obstruction to the prime numbers as a spectrum of a probability measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12400)
*Zhi-Yi Wu,Qian Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了：在波利尼亚克猜想（每个足够大的正偶数都可表示为两个素数之差）成立的条件下，素数集合P不可能成为任何实数上Borel概率测度的频谱（即L²(μ)中指数正交基的频率集合）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索素数在调和分析与算子理论中的结构性限制，揭示素数集合作为指数正交基（频谱）不可能性的数论条件，将数论猜想与测度谱理论联系起来。

Method: 利用素数差猜想（波利尼亚克猜想的特殊情况）作为核心数论前提，结合Borel概率测度的正交基理论，通过反证法证明若素数可构成某测度的频谱，则会导致与该数论猜想相矛盾的结论。

Result: 证明了素数集合P在任何概率测度下都不能构成L²(μ)的指数正交基（即频谱），该结论依赖于波利尼亚克猜想这一数论假设。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了数论猜想与测度谱理论之间的深刻联系：在波利尼亚克猜想下，素数集合在结构上无法作为任何概率测度的频谱，这是对Minkowski问题中频谱构造的一类重要否定性结果。

Abstract: Let $\Pp=\{2,3,5,7,\ldots\}$ denote the set of prime numbers. We prove that if every sufficiently large positive even integer can be represented as a difference of two primes, then there is no Borel probability measure $μ$ on $\R$ for which \(\left\{e^{2πi p x}:p\in\Pp\right\}\) is an orthonormal basis of $L^2(μ)$. In particular, under the Polignac conjecture, the prime numbers $\Pp$ cannot be a spectrum (i.e., the set of frequencies of an exponential orthonormal basis) of any probability measure on $\R$.

</details>


### [111] [Finding all cospectral mates over a number field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12410)
*Alexander Van Werde*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了整数矩阵的一种由代数数域参数化的共谱性概念，探讨了在给定数域中通过正交矩阵共轭能否产生新的整数矩阵，并给出了谱确定性的充分条件及相关约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的共谱性问题关注整数矩阵的本质共谱性（由有理正交矩阵共轭得到），但对有限域或具体数域参数化的共谱性缺少系统性研究。作者希望理解在给定数域下，整数矩阵通过正交变换能产生哪些新的整数矩阵，从而推广谱确定理论。

Method: 利用特征多项式的判别式和Krylov子空间的性质来推导谱确定性的充分条件；在不满足条件的情形下给出正交矩阵的约束；并基于该理论设计了一个在给定（小）数域上寻找所有共谱伴侣的算法，且提供了实现代码。

Result: 得到了与数域相关的共谱性概念的充分条件，给出了当条件不适用时正交矩阵的约束描述，并成功开发了可在给定小域上枚举所有共谱伴侣的算法及实现。

Conclusion: 该研究为整数矩阵在代数数域参数化下的共谱性问题提供了理论工具和计算算法，扩展了谱确定性的研究框架，并为后续在算术和图论中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate a notion of cospectrality for integer matrices that is parameterized by algebraic number fields. Given a number field and a symmetric integer matrix, we wonder when conjugating the integer matrix by an orthogonal matrix with entries in the given field can produce new integer matrices.
  Our results concern sufficient conditions for the associated notion of spectral determination, and we give constraints on the orthogonal matrices when the conditions are not applicable. The results use the discriminant of the characteristic polynomial and properties of Krylov subspaces. We leverage the theory to develop an algorithm to find all cospectral mates over a given (small) field. An implementation of the algorithm is made available.

</details>


### [112] [Special Values of Shifted Dirichlet Series from an Adjoint Map on Almost Holomorphic Modular Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12542)
*Raveena Ganash*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文找到了几乎全纯模形式空间上一个映射的伴随算子，该映射类似于准模形式空间上的广义Ramanujan-Serre导数。作为应用，作者显式地求出了与准模形式相关的某些移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值，并证明了移位Dirichlet级数的有理性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Ramanujan-Serre导数在准模形式理论中具有重要作用，但缺乏与其对应的伴随算子的系统构造。作者希望在几乎全纯模形式空间上建立类似的伴随算子理论，从而为研究移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值和有理性提供新工具。

Method: 本文在几乎全纯模形式空间上构造了一个映射的伴随算子，利用该伴随算子类比广义Ramanujan-Serre导数的结构，并通过该算子显式计算特定移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值，进而证明其有理性。

Result: 成功构造了几乎全纯模形式空间上的伴随算子，显式求出了与准模形式相关的某些移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值，并证明了这些移位Dirichlet级数的有理性。

Conclusion: 该工作将Ramanujan-Serre导数理论推广到几乎全纯模形式框架下，为移位Dirichlet级数提供了新的精确值与有理性结果，进一步深化了模形式与Dirichlet级数之间的联系。

Abstract: We find the adjoint of a map on the space of almost holomorphic modular forms, analogous to the generalized Ramanujan-Serre derivative on the space of quasi-modular forms. As applications, we explicitly find special values of certain shifted Dirichlet series associated to quasi-modular forms and prove a rationality result for the shifted Dirichlet series.

</details>


### [113] [Finite-Sum Realization of Archimedean Asai and Exterior-Square $L$-Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12722)
*Yeongseong Jo,Akash Yadav*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了阿基米德局部域上的Asai L-因子可表示为Flicker局部zeta积分的有限和，同时证明了外平方L-因子可表示为Jacquet-Shalika局部zeta积分的有限和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在朗兰兹纲领中，L-函数的解析性质（如函数方程和解析延拓）是核心问题。通过局部zeta积分来表示L-因子，是研究其解析性质的重要途径。该论文旨在将Asai L-因子和外平方L-因子在阿基米德情形下纳入Flicker和Jacquet-Shalika局部zeta积分的框架中。

Method: 论文采用解析数论与表示论的方法。对于GL_n(C)上不可约generic表示，建立了阿基米德Asai L-因子与Flicker局部zeta积分的有限和表达式；对于阿基米德局部域F上GL_m(F)的不可约generic表示，建立了外平方L-因子与Jacquet-Shalika局部zeta积分的有限和表达式。证明可能涉及Whittaker模型、局部L-因子的具体公式以及对zeta积分收敛性和函子性性质的细致分析。

Result: 主要结果为两个定理：(1) GL_n(C)上的Asai L-因子可表达为有限个Flicker局部zeta积分之和；(2) 阿基米德局部域上GL_m的外平方L-因子可表达为有限个Jacquet-Shalika局部zeta积分之和。

Conclusion: 该工作将两类重要的L-因子在阿基米德情形下与经典的局部zeta积分理论建立了联系，为后续研究这些L-因子的函数方程、特殊值以及一般化拓展提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We prove that the archimedean Asai $L$-factor attached to an irreducible generic representation of $\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ can be expressed as a finite sum of Flicker local zeta integrals. For an archimedean local field $F$, we also prove that the exterior-square $L$-factor attached to an irreducible generic representation of $\operatorname{GL}_m(F)$ can be expressed as a finite sum of Jacquet--Shalika local zeta integrals.

</details>


### [114] [Density bounds for permutations avoiding monotone arithmetic progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12604)
*Jesse Geneson*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了整数集和自然数集上容许ω-排列（不含单调ℓ项算术级数）的子集的极大上、下密度。主要结果包括：证明α_N(3)≥2/3和α_Z(3)≥2/3，加强了已发表的关于三项上密度参数的下界；并通过构造四可排列的整数子集，证明β_Z(4)=1，即其下对称密度可趋近于1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于不含单调算术级数的ω-排列的存在性，已有研究中给出的三项上密度下界不够强。作者旨在加强这些下界，并解决四可排列子集的密度问题，以深化对整数和自然数子集结构性质的理解。

Method: 通过构造具体子集和排列来证明密度下界：对于α_N(3)和α_Z(3)，构造具有足够高上密度的子集，使其容许不含单调三项算术级数的ω-排列；对于β_Z(4)=1，构造四可排列的整数子集，使其下对称密度趋近于1。

Result: 证明α_N(3)≥2/3且α_Z(3)≥2/3，加强了已发表的三项上密度参数下界；证明β_Z(4)=1，即存在整数子集容许不含单调四项算术级数的ω-排列，且其下对称密度可任意接近1。

Conclusion: 本文在算术级数与排列密度参数的交叉领域取得了实质性进展，显著加强了已知下界并完全确定了β_Z(4)的值。

Abstract: For $X\in\{\mathbb{N},\mathbb{Z}\}$, let $α_X(\ell)$ and $β_X(\ell)$ denote the supremal upper and lower densities of subsets of $X$ admitting $ω$-permutations without monotone $\ell$-term arithmetic progressions. We strengthen the published lower bounds for the three-term upper-density parameters by proving \[
  α_{\mathbb{N}}(3)\geq\frac23,\qquad
  α_{\mathbb{Z}}(3)\geq\frac23. \] We also prove $β_{\mathbb{Z}}(4)=1$ by constructing four-permutable subsets of the integers whose lower symmetric densities approach one.

</details>


### [115] [Escalations and criteria over real quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12648)
*Jakub Krásenský,Giuliano Romeo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了数域上普遍二次型的标准集（criterion sets），将经典的15-定理和290-定理推广到全实数域。作者发展了数域上的"升级方法"（escalation method），给出了标准集有限性的简洁证明，并通过计算机计算得到了Q(√2)和Q(√3)上的普遍性判据猜想，同时为Q(√5)建立了对角二次型的标准集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典15-定理和290-定理完全刻画了有理数域上的普遍二次型，但类似定理虽然对每个全实数域都存在，除了Q(√5)外其标准集（criterion set）并不显式已知。作者希望发展一种既有理论价值又具计算可行性的方法，以确定其他数域上的普遍性判据。

Method: 作者将升级方法（escalation method）推广到数域上。该方法通过逐步构造"升级格"（escalator lattices）来生成标准集，并从理论上证明了标准集的有限性。此外，作者还发展了一套针对对角二次型的升级理论，用以处理对角情形。计算方面，作者对Q(√2)和Q(√3)进行了显式计算。

Result: 对Q(√2)和Q(√3)进行了显式计算，获得了关于相应普遍性判据的猜想；证明了许多升级格的普遍性；为Q(√5)建立了对角标准集，并为Q(√2)和Q(√3)给出了对角标准集的猜想。

Conclusion: 升级方法在数域上不仅是证明标准集有限性的有效理论工具，而且是计算标准集的实用方法。它为系统确定更多全实数域上的普遍性判据提供了技术路线，也为对角二次型情形提供了独立的理论框架。

Abstract: The famous 15-Theorem and 290-Theorem fully characterise universal quadratic forms over $\mathbb{Q}$. Similar theorems exist for every totally real number field, but only over $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt5)$ the criterion set is explicitly known. We study criterion sets both theoretically and computationally: We develop the method of escalation over number fields, thus providing a simple proof of finiteness of the criteria and, more importantly, a practical tool for computing them. We illustrate this by explicit computations for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$ and $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt3)$, obtaining a conjecture about the corresponding universality criteria; we also prove universality of many escalator lattices. Moreover, we develop the somewhat different theory of escalations for diagonal quadratic forms, obtaining the diagonal criterion set for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt5)$ and conjecturally for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$ and $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt3)$.

</details>


### [116] [Algebraic constructions of point sequences with quasi-uniform two-dimensional projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12690)
*Takashi Goda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了d维单位立方体中可扩展点集的代数构造，其所有二维坐标投影都是准均匀的（即网格比一致有界）。提出了两类构造：基于三次数域的Kronecker序列和基于实二次域与p-adic嵌入的嵌套秩1格点设计，两者统一地用射影线P¹(Q)上的有理方向有限构型来参数化，并通过代数范数估计与转置原理证明了最优阶的分离半径下界和覆盖半径上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受序贯空间填充设计的启发，研究者希望构造可扩展的点集（即初始段可逐步扩展），使得在任意二维坐标投影下仍保持优良的均匀性。传统空间填充设计往往难以同时满足可扩展性和多维投影均匀性，因此需要代数数论工具来系统构造这类点集。

Method: 1) 利用三次数域构造显式Kronecker序列，使得所有二维坐标投影的网格比在任意长度N≥2的初始段上一致有界；2) 利用实二次域、分裂素数和相容的p-adic嵌入构造嵌套秩1格点设计，在每个嵌套层级保持相同的均匀投影性质。证明中结合代数范数估计与联立/对偶Diophantine逼近之间的转置原理，对分离半径和覆盖半径分别给出最优阶的下界与上界；3) 提出关于P¹(Q)上有理投影系数选取的极小极大问题，用于最小化所有二维坐标投影上的最大网格比。

Result: 成功构造了两类可扩展点集（Kronecker序列和嵌套秩1格点设计），它们的所有二维坐标投影均为准均匀的，且分离半径和覆盖半径的界达到了点数的最优阶。同时揭示了有理射影系数与网格比之间的内在联系，将其归结为一个关于P¹(Q)上有限构型的极小极大优化问题。

Conclusion: 通过代数数论（三次数域和实二次域）为可扩展空间填充设计提供了新的构造框架，这些点集在任意二维投影下都具有一致受控的双变量几何结构，为序贯计算机实验设计提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Motivated by sequential space-filling designs for computer experiments, we study algebraic constructions of extensible point sets in the $d$-dimensional unit cube whose two-dimensional coordinate projections are all quasi-uniform. Our two constructions share a common parametrization in terms of finite configurations of distinct rational directions on the projective line $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q})$. First, using a cubic number field, we construct explicit Kronecker sequences for which the mesh ratios of all two-dimensional coordinate projections remain uniformly bounded over every initial segment of length $N\ge 2$. Second, using a real quadratic field, a split prime, and a compatible $p$-adic embedding, we construct nested rank-1 lattice designs with the same uniform projection property at every nesting level. The proofs combine algebraic norm estimates with transference principles between simultaneous and dual Diophantine approximation, yielding lower bounds for the separation radii and upper bounds for the covering radii, both of optimal order in the number of points, uniformly over all coordinate pairs. We also investigate how the choice of rational projective coefficients affects the resulting mesh ratios. This leads to a minimax problem for finite configurations on $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q})$, in which one seeks to minimize the maximum mesh ratio over all two-dimensional coordinate projections. These constructions provide extensible point sets with uniformly controlled bivariate geometry.

</details>


### [117] [Uniform bounds on prime levels of abelian division fields of elliptic curves over number fields without rationally defined CM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12709)
*Hansol Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文去除了广义黎曼猜想（GRH）假设，证明了对于无理性定义复乘法（CM）的椭圆曲线E/K，其素数级division域在K上为阿贝尔这一性质等价于E不具有理性定义的CM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: González-Jiménez和Lozano-Robledo证明了椭圆曲线E/Q的阿贝尔n-division域存在一致上界。Allen和Genao在GRH假设下将该结果部分推广到任意数域（限制在素数级，且排除理性定义CM的情形）。本文的目标是移除GRH假设，使该结果成为无条件定理。

Method: 通过精细化的Galois表示与自同构群分析，构建素数级division域阿贝尔性的充要条件，并证明该阿贝尔性质成立当且仅当椭圆曲线不具有理性定义的CM。关键思路是利用CM与非CM情形下Galois表示的不变量结构差异，结合局部-整体原理完成无GRH的证明。

Result: 成功移除了GRH假设，证明了Allen和Genao所建立的关于素数级division域阿贝尔性的性质，在任意数域上无条件地等价于椭圆曲线不具有理性定义的CM。

Conclusion: 该结果将阿贝尔division域的有界性问题从Q推广到任意数域，并完全刻画了此类性质与CM结构之间的等价关系，且在无GRH假设的宽松条件下成立。

Abstract: Enrique Gonz{á}lez-Jim{é}nez and {Á}lvaro Lozano-Robledo proved that there exists a uniform bound on the levels $n$ for which the $n$-division field $\mathbb{Q}\left(E\left[n\right]\right) / \mathbb{Q}$ of an elliptic curve $E/\mathbb{Q}$ defined over $\mathbb{Q}$ is abelian. Assuming GRH (Generalized Riemann Hypothesis), Allen and Genao partially generalized this result by restricting to prime levels while allowing arbitrary number fields without rationally defined CM. In this paper, we remove the GRH assumption and prove that the property established by Allen and Genao is in fact equivalent to the absence of rationally defined CM.

</details>


### [118] [On the Lorenz-Fibonacci sequences and substitutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12739)
*Bernardo San Martín,Víctor Sirvent*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了$(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci整数序列的两族序列（第一类与第二类），其特征多项式为$p_{k,r}(x)$，并引入了与这些序列相关联的两个字母替换（substitutions）族，随后研究了这些替换的主要组合性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这些序列自然地出现在Lorenz吸引子动力学的研究中，且包含了著名的$k$-bonacci序列（当$r=0$时）作为特例，具有重要的数学与应用价值。

Method: 定义了第一类和第二类$(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci整数序列，其特征多项式为$p_{k,r}(x)=x^k-x^{k-1}-\cdots-x^r+x^{r-1}+\cdots+x+1$，其中$k\geq 2r+1$且$k\geq 3$。在由$k$个符号组成的字母表上构造了两族替换，使它们分别与两类整数序列相关联并共享同一特征多项式，进而研究这些替换的组合性质（如平衡性、复杂度等）。

Result: 成功构造了两族与$(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci序列相关联的替换，并系统研究了这些替换的主要组合性质。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了Lorenz-Fibonacci整数序列与符号动力学中替换理论之间的联系，推广了经典的$k$-bonacci情况，为Lorenz吸引子动力学的组合学研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: In the present article, we consider two families of integer sequences, the $(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci sequences of the first and second kind, whose characteristic polynomial is $$p_{k,r}(x)=x^k-x^{k-1}-\cdots- x^r+x^{r-1}+\cdots +x+1,$$ where $k\geq 2r+1$, and $k\geq 3$. These families include the well-known $k$-bonacci sequence, when $r=0$. These sequences arise naturally in the study of the dynamics of the Lorenz attractor. We introduce two families of substitutions (in an alphabet of $k$ symbols) so that they are associated with each of the families of integer sequences and share the same polynomial. We study the main combinatorial properties of these substitutions.

</details>


### [119] [Cyclic permutations of large subsets with polynomial values in multiplicative subgroups of finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12758)
*Hai-Liang Wu,He-Xia Ni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域$\mathbb{F}_p$上任意子集$A$的某种哈密顿环问题：给定多项式$f(t)\in\mathbb{Z}[t]$和整数$k\ge2$，证明了当素$p\equiv1\pmod{k}$足够大时，存在一个阈值$c(p,k,f)$，使得任何大小超过该阈值的子集$A$都能排列成一个环$a_1,\dots,a_{\#A}$，满足相邻两数（环绕求和）经$f$映射后为$k$次幂剩余。同时给出了该阈值的最佳上下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决有限域上序列排列的代数约束问题——即寻找一个排列使得相邻元素之和在给定多项式映射下落入$k$次幂剩余集合。这类问题联系了数论（幂剩余）、加性组合学和图论（哈密顿圈存在性），具有理论交叉意义，并可能对有限域上的构造性问题有应用价值。

Method: 论文综合运用了三种技术工具：1）有限域上的混合指数和（mixed exponential sums）来估计加性组合结构的伪随机性；2）离散傅里叶分析来处理幂剩余特征的分布；3）谱图理论来建立图哈密顿性与其谱性质之间的关联，从而推导出阈值的存在性及其上下界。

Result: 作者对于每个足够大的素数$p\equiv1\pmod{k}$，构造性地给出了阈值$c(p,k,f)$，使得任意满足$\#A\ge c(p,k,f)$的子集$A\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p$都存在满足题设条件的排列（即形成$f$-幂剩余的哈密顿圈）。此外，作者还证明了该最小可能阈值具有明确的下界和上界。

Conclusion: 本文在一般的多项式$f$和指数$k$的情形下，建立了有限域子集在$k$次幂剩余约束下存在"好排列"的充分条件（以阈值形式给出），并刻画了该阈值的最优范围。这推广了此前可能只针对特定情形（如$f(t)=t$或其他特殊多项式）的已知结果，为有限域上的结构化排列问题提供了统一的框架性解答。

Abstract: Let $f(t)\in\mathbb{Z}[t]$ be a nonconstant polynomial with nonzero discriminant and let $k\ge2$ be an integer. For every
  sufficiently large prime $p\equiv1\pmod{k}$, by applying mixed exponential sums over finite fields, discrete Fourier analysis and the spectral graph theory, we establish a threshold $c(p,k,f)$ such that any subset $A\subseteq\mathbb F_p$ with $\#A\ge c(p,k,f)$ admits a permutation $a_1, a_2,\cdots, a_{\#A}$ of $A$ satisfying $f(a_i+a_{i+1})\in\{x^k: x\in\mathbb{F}_p^*\}$ for any $1\le i \le \#A$, where $a_{\#A+1}=a_1$. Also, we give lower and upper bounds for the least possible threshold.

</details>


### [120] [A study of degenerate Bernoulli and Euler numbers via operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12927)
*Taekyun Kim,Dae san Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入新型算子并将其应用于特殊数的研究，利用这些算子推导退化欧拉数和第2类退化欧拉数的显式表达式，同时建立了退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过引入新型算子这一数学工具，为特殊数（特别是退化欧拉数和退化伯努利数）的研究提供新的视角和方法，从而简化和统一这些数的显式表达式推导过程，并揭示它们之间的内在联系。

Method: 本文的核心方法是引入一类新型算子，利用这些算子系统地推导退化欧拉数和第2类退化欧拉数的显式表达式，并在此基础上进一步建立退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的数学关系。

Result: 成功推导出退化欧拉数和第2类退化欧拉数的显式表达式，并建立了退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的关联关系。这些算子被证明在研究此类显式表达式和关系中自然涌现。

Conclusion: 新型算子在退化特殊数的研究中具有重要的方法论价值，不仅能够简化显式表达式的推导过程，还能揭示退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的深层联系，为后续相关研究提供了新的工具和思路。

Abstract: The paper introduces novel classes of operators and investigates their applications to the study of special numbers. We utilize these operators to derive explicit expressions for degenerate Euler numbers and type 2 degenerate Euler numbers. Furthermore, we establish a relationship between degenerate Bernoulli numbers and degenerate Euler numbers. We note here that these operators emerge naturally when we study such explicit expressions and such a relationship

</details>


### [121] [Analytic rank one propagation in Hida families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13145)
*Stefano Vigni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于模形式p-adic Hida族中解析秩（analytic rank）的minimality猜想的结果：在若干算术几何猜想成立的前提下，若一个非CM新形式f的解析秩为1，则几乎所有通过f的Hida族中偶权平凡Nebentypus特化也有解析秩1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Greenberg提出了关于模形式族中解析秩的"minimality猜想"，即在Hida族中解析秩在族中是"最小化"的——若族中某一点的解析秩为1，则几乎所有点的解析秩都为1。该猜想与Bloch-Kato猜想、Iwasawa理论密切相关，但目前缺乏一般性进展，本文旨在在特定假设下为这个猜想提供证据。

Method: 本文的方法基于p-adic Hida理论。设f为非CM新形式，p为f的ordinary素数，构造通过f的p-adic Hida族f^(p)。利用p-adic Abel-Jacobi映射的单射性和archimedean高度配对（Gillet-Soulé意义下）的正定性这两个算术代数几何中的一般性猜想，对解析秩为1的f，证明Hida族中几乎所有偶权平凡Nebentypus特化也具有解析秩1。

Result: 在p-adic Abel-Jacobi映射单射性和Gillet-Soulé archimedean高度配对正定性的特定实例成立的假设下，对几乎所有（除有限多个外）这样的ordinary素数p，若f的解析秩为1，则Hida族f^(p)中所有但有限多个偶权平凡Nebentypus特化的解析秩也为1。

Conclusion: 该结果为Greenberg关于模形式族中解析秩的"minimality猜想"提供了支持性证据，表明在特定条件下Hida族内解析秩分布遵循最小化原则。这为进一步研究p-adic族中L-函数零点分布和Selmer秩的变化提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Let $f$ be a non-CM newform of weight $k\geq4$, level $N$ and trivial Nebentypus. Let $p\nmid N$ be an odd prime number that is ordinary for $f$ and denote by $\boldsymbol f^{(p)}$ the $p$-adic Hida family passing through $f$. Assuming very specific instances of two general conjectures in arithmetic algebraic geometry (injectivity of $p$-adic Abel-Jacobi maps, positive definiteness of archimedean height pairings à la Gillet-Soulé), we prove that, for all but finitely many $p$ as above, if the analytic rank of $f$ is $1$, then all but finitely many specializations of $\boldsymbol f^{(p)}$ of even weight and trivial Nebentypus have analytic rank $1$. This result provides evidence for Greenberg's "minimality conjecture" on analytic ranks in families of modular forms.

</details>


### [122] [On $\mathcal{B}^4$-almost periodicity for a class of arithmetical functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13172)
*Kritika Aggarwal,Debika Banerjee,Shubham Gupta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了Chandrasekharan-Narasimhan算术函数类相关归一化误差项的B^4-几乎周期性（Besicovitch意义下），显著强于以往文献中的B^2-几乎周期性结果，并由此推导出该误差项的极限概率分布和平均四次幂矩的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献中仅研究了相关误差项的B^2-几乎周期性，本文旨在将这一结果显著加强到B^4-几乎周期性，以更深入地理解这类广泛算术函数类误差项的分布性质。

Method: 通过推导截断的Voronoï型公式，证明归一化误差项属于Besicovitch空间B^4，从而建立B^4-几乎周期性。

Result: 证明了归一化误差项具有B^4-几乎周期性，进而推论出该误差项存在极限概率分布，并给出了其平均四次幂矩的显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文的B^4-几乎周期性结果为该广泛算术函数类误差项的统计性质提供了更强有力的刻画，并揭示了其更深层的概率分布结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the $\mathcal{B}^4$-almost periodicity in the sense of Besicovitch for a suitably normalized error term associated with a broad class of arithmetical functions introduced by Chandrasekharan and Narasimhan. This result significantly strengthens $\mathcal{B}^2$-almost periodicity previously investigated in the literature for related error terms. By deriving truncated Voronoï-type formulas, we demonstrate that the normalized error term lies in the Besicovitch space $B^4$. As a consequence, we deduce that the error term admits a limit probability distribution and establish an explicit formula for its mean fourth power moment.

</details>


### [123] [Generalizations of the Christoffel-Darboux formula and congruences involving Apéry-like numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13192)
*Zhi-Hong Sun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首先将正交多项式的 Christoffel-Darboux 公式推广到一般三項递推序列，然后研究由两个给定递推关系定义的 g_n(x) 和 v_n(x) 的恒等式与同余性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christoffel-Darboux 公式是正交多项式理论中的基本工具，将其推广到更一般的三項递推序列可以拓展其适用范围，并用于研究特定递推序列（如 g_n(x) 和 v_n(x)）的性质。

Method: 首先将 Christoffel-Darboux 公式推广至一般三項递推序列，然后利用该推广研究由特定递推关系定义的 g_n(x) 与 v_n(x) 序列，推导其恒等式并建立相关同余性质。

Result: 得到了推广的 Christoffel-Darboux 公式，并将其应用于 g_n(x) 和 v_n(x) 两个序列，推导出相应的恒等式和同余结果。

Conclusion: 通过推广 Christoffel-Darboux 公式，成功建立了对一般三項递推序列的分析框架，并为 g_n(x)、v_n(x) 序列给出了新的恒等式与同余性质。

Abstract: In this paper, we first extend the Christoffel-Darboux formula for orthogonal polynomials to general three-term recurrence sequences, and then investigate the identities and congruences for $g_n(x)$ and $v_n(x)$ given by \begin{align*} &g_0(x)=1,\ g_1(x)=\frac{x+1}2,\ (n+1)^2g_{n+1}(x)=\Big(2n(n+1)+\frac{x+1}2\Big)g_n(x)-n^2g_{n-1}(x)\ (n\ge 1), \\&v_0(x)=1,\ v_1(x)=x,\ (n+1)^3v_{n+1}(x)=(2n+1)(n(n+1)+x)v_n(x)-n^3v_{n-1}(x)\ (n\ge 1).\end{align*}

</details>


### [124] [Infinitely many elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ of exact ranks 4 and 6 with $j$-invariant 1728](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13202)
*Ben Savoie*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对 r∈{4,6}，在数域 Q(i) 上构造了显式的单参数椭圆曲线族，其 j-不变量为 1728，且秩恰好等于 r，族中包含无穷多条彼此不同构的、真正定义在 Q(i) 上的椭圆曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前作者已在秩为2的情形下建立了相应构造，本文旨在将策略推广至更高秩（秩4和秩6），以证明在高秩情形下仍能构造具有固定 j=1728 的显式椭圆曲线族，并精确确定其秩。

Method: （1）构造显式的 Q(i)-有理点以给出秩的下界；（2）利用 Kai 关于数域上线性模式的素数值定理进行特殊化，控制局部行为；（3）通过 [1+i]-下降（descent）给出秩的上界，使上下界匹配；（4）将原作者秩2论文中用对称高斯素数构型的方法替换为满足若干互补平方恒等式的二元线性形式系统；（5）在秩6情形中，三个构造点与 (0,0) 的支撑向量张成自对偶 Reed-Muller 码 RM(1,3)，该码同时作为控制 Selmer 群的二次剩余 Laplacian 的核出现。

Result: 成功构造了秩恰好为4和6的显式单参数椭圆曲线族，定义了无穷多条彼此不同构、真正定义在 Q(i) 上且 j=1728 的椭圆曲线，并通过下降与特殊化给出了匹配的上下界。

Conclusion: 该工作将此前秩2的结果推广到秩4和秩6，展示了利用线性形式系统、素数值定理与 Reed-Muller 码等工具精确控制椭圆曲线秩的可行性，为进一步构造更高秩曲线提供了新的方法框架。

Abstract: For each $r\in\{4,6\}$, we construct an explicit one-parameter family of elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ containing infinitely many pairwise nonisomorphic curves genuinely defined over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ with $j$-invariant $1728$ and rank exactly $r$. We construct explicit $\mathbb{Q}(i)$-rational points to bound the ranks from below. Kai's theorem on prime values of linear patterns over number fields provides specializations with controlled local behavior, allowing us to obtain matching upper bounds via $[1+i]$-descent. The construction extends the strategy of the author's earlier rank-$2$ paper by replacing a symmetric Gaussian-prime configuration with systems of binary linear forms satisfying several complementary square identities. In the rank-$6$ case, the support vectors attached to the three constructed points and $(0,0)$ span the self-dual Reed-Muller code $\mathrm{RM}(1,3)$, which also occurs as the kernel of the quadratic-residue Laplacian governing the Selmer group.

</details>


### [125] [Asymptotic Analysis and Phase Transition of the Bessel-Kuznetsov Transform with an Oscillatory Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13232)
*Yuhang Shi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对GL(2)的Kuznetsov迹公式中的Bessel-Kuznetsov积分变换$\check\phi(t)$在含高度振荡线性相位$e(\alpha x)$时的半经典极限$t\to\infty$进行了严格且显式的渐近分析，利用WKB近似揭示了由扭转参数$\alpha$决定的尖锐相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典界只能保证光滑非振荡测试函数的快速衰减，但现代解析数论应用（特别是扭转移位卷积和问题）中常遇到高度振荡线性相位的测试函数，需要精确了解其半经典极限行为。

Method: 对虚阶Bessel核应用WKB近似，在$t\to\infty$的半经典极限下对$\check\phi(t)$进行严格显式的渐近展开，并根据扭转参数$\alpha$将问题划分为亚临界与超临界两个区域。

Result: 证明了：在亚临界区域（$\alpha\le 1/2\pi$）变换快速衰减；在超临界区域（$\alpha>1/2\pi$），几何振荡与谱核共振，产生一个阶为$O(t^{-1})$的局部化主项，且其算术相位显著简化。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了Kuznetsov变换在振荡测试函数下的精确渐近行为，确立了由$\alpha=1/2\pi$控制的尖锐相变，为涉及扭转移位卷积和的解析数论问题提供了新的工具。

Abstract: The spectral side of the Kuznetsov trace formula for $GL(2)$ is governed by the Bessel-Kuznetsov integral transform $\checkφ(t)$. While classical bounds guarantee rapid decay of this transform for smooth, non-oscillatory test functions, modern applications in analytic number theory---particularly those involving twisted shifted convolution sums---frequently encounter test functions exhibiting a highly oscillatory linear phase $e(αx)$. In this paper, we provide a rigorous and explicit asymptotic analysis of $\checkφ(t)$ in the semiclassical limit $t \to \infty$ under such oscillatory conditions. By applying the WKB approximation to the imaginary-order Bessel kernel, we identify a sharp phase transition dependent on the twist parameter $α$. We prove that in the sub-critical regime ($α\le 1/2π$), the transform decays rapidly. Conversely, in the super-critical regime ($α> 1/2π$), the geometric oscillations resonate with the spectral kernel, yielding a localized main term of order $O(t^{-1})$ with a remarkably simplified arithmetic phase.

</details>


### [126] [On a conjecture of Corradi and Katai](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13266)
*Krishnarjun Krishnamoorthy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了与Liouville函数相关的Goldbach型求和，证明了充分的抵消现象存在，并将其应用于Liouville函数的符号模式研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldbach型求和是数论中的经典问题，而Liouville函数作为重要的算术函数，其在Goldbach型求和中的抵消行为直接关系到素数分布与相关猜想的研究。此外，Liouville函数的符号模式分析对于理解其伪随机性质具有重要意义。

Method: 论文通过分析Liouville函数相关的Goldbach型求和，建立了充分抵消的证明，并利用这一结果进一步研究Liouville函数中符号模式的结构与性质。

Result: 成功证明了Liouville函数对应的Goldbach型求和中存在充分的抵消，并将其应用于Liouville函数符号模式的深入分析。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅推进了Goldbach型求和问题的理解，还为Liouville函数符号模式提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: We consider Goldbach type sums corresponding to the Liouville function and prove the existence of sufficient cancellations. We also consider applications to sign patterns in the Liouville function.

</details>


### [127] [Convolution-type Bombieri-Vinogradov theorem with well-factorable Weights, and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13299)
*Zhiyuan Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Pascadi的三重良分解卷积估计和不完全Kloosterman和估计，将Bombieri-Friedlander-Iwaniec关于素数在算术级数中均匀分布到模$x^{4/7-\varepsilon}$的经典结果推广到卷积形式，并应用于研究最大素因子相关问题，得到了两个改进的数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bombieri-Friedlander-Iwaniec在1986年证明了素数在算术级数中具有均匀分布性（模数可达$x^{4/7-\varepsilon}$），本文的动机是将这一经典结果推广到卷积形式，并利用此推广来改进此前关于最大素因子相关密度的数值估计。

Method: 本文方法的核心是应用Pascadi提出的三重良分解卷积估计（triply-well-factorable convolution estimate）及不完全Kloosterman和的估计，将BFI的经典结果推广为卷积形式。与Wang的结果相比，在特定条件下有所改进。然后利用该卷积形式结果分析$P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)$与$P^+(p-1)\geq p^c$两类去重计数问题的渐近密度。

Result: 得到两个主要结果：(1) $\#\{n\leq x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)\}>0.296x$，改进了作者此前得到的0.280；(2) $\limsup_{x\to\infty}\frac{1}{\pi(x)}\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}\leq S(c)$，其中$S(c)<\frac{7}{2}\log\frac{1}{c}$，改善Ding和Wang（2025）得到的上界$\frac{7}{2}\log\frac{1}{c}$。

Conclusion: 通过将BFI定理推广到卷积形式，本文在两类涉及最大素因子的数论计数问题上取得了数值上的改进，验证了Pascadi方法在此类问题中的有效性，并为后续相关研究提供了更精细的分析工具。

Abstract: In 1986, Bombieri, Friedlander and Iwaniec famously obtained that primes are equidistributed in arithmetic progressions to moduli up to $x^{4/7-\varepsilon}$, using well-factorable weights. In this paper, we apply Pascadi's triply-well-factorable convolution estimate and his estimation of incomplete Kloosterman sums to generalize this result to a convolutionform, which improves Wang's result under certain conditions. As for application, we consider the asymptotic density of $\#\{n\leq x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)\}$ and $\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}$, where $P^+(n)$ denote the largest prime factor of $n$. We show that for $x\rightarrow\infty$, one has\begin{align*}
  \#\{n\leq x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)\}>0.296x
  \end{align*}
  and
  \begin{align*}
  &\mathop{\lim\sup}_{x\rightarrow\infty} \frac{1}{π(x)}\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}\\&\leq S(c)=\left\{ \begin{aligned}
  & \int_0^{1-c}\frac{2}{(5/8-189u/200)(1-u)}\mathrm{d}u, \quad&& \frac{184}{189}\leq c<1,\\& S\left(\frac{184}{189}\right)+\frac{10}{3}\log\frac{184}{189c},\quad&& 0.7404<c\leq \frac{184}{189},
  \end{aligned}\right.
  \end{align*}
  where the function $S(c)$ satisfties $S(c)<\frac{7}{2}\log\frac{1}{c} $ for $0.7404<c<1$. The first result improves a previous result $0.280$ by the author (2026). The second result constitutes an improvement upon that of Ding and Wang (2025), who obatined $\mathop{\lim\sup}_{x\rightarrow\infty} \frac{1}{π(x)}\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}\leq \frac{7}{2}\log\frac{1}{c}$.

</details>


### [128] [Local Minkowski units in non-abelian extensions with cyclic Sylow $p$-subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13312)
*Wan Lee,Donghyeok Lim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了在所有伽罗瓦扩张（其伽罗瓦群同构于非p群与循环p群的直积）上局部Minkowski单位在p处存在性的判定准则，并以此为工具构造了多种分歧条件下的非交换扩张、分析了数域Z_p扩张中单位的岩泽模结构，还将研究扩展到了p次单位根群非平凡的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部Minkowski单位在代数数论中具有重要地位，但现有研究对其在更一般伽罗瓦扩张中的存在性缺乏系统性判定条件。作者希望建立适用于更广泛扩张类型的判别准则，并探明其在不同算术场景中的推论。

Method: 通过将伽罗瓦群分解为非p群与循环p群的直积，利用群论与局部类域论相结合的方法建立存在性判据；该判据依次应用于构造非交换扩张、分析Z_p扩张中单位的岩泽模结构，并推广至p次单位根群非平凡的情形。

Result: 获得了适用于上述群结构下局部Minkowski单位存在性的完整判定条件，成功构造了多种分歧条件下承认局部Minkowski单位的非交换扩张，并对Z_p扩张中单位的岩泽模结构给出了分析结果，同时将结论推广至含p次单位根的情况。

Conclusion: 本文的结果为局部Minkowski单位的存在性问题提供了统一且广泛适用的理论框架，深化了对该算术对象在非交换伽罗瓦扩张及岩泽理论中作用的理解。

Abstract: We establish a criterion for the existence of a local Minkowski unit at $p$ that applies to all Galois extensions with Galois group isomorphic to the direct product of a non-$p$-group and a cyclic $p$-group. As applications, we construct non-abelian extensions admitting a local Minkowski unit at $p$ under various ramification conditions and analyze the Iwasawa module structure of units in $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extensions of number fields. We also extend our study to the case where the group of $p$-power roots of unity is nontrivial.

</details>


### [129] [Some convolution identities for mock modular forms arising from the theory of holomorphic projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13324)
*Jonathan G. Bradley-Thrush,Frank Garvan,Jayashree Kalita,Larry Rolen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文发展了全纯投影的新结果，推广了Imamoğlu、Raum和Richter关于调和Maass形式与全纯模形式乘积的全纯投影理论，从广义Pell方程有有限解的情形扩展到一般D（包括Pell方程有无穷多解的情形），并作为应用解决了第二作者的一个猜想，得到了18个mock theta函数的卷积恒等式（可简化为5个核心恒等式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全纯模形式理论中长期存在卷积恒等式和递推公式的研究，这些公式在组合学中有重要应用。近年来非全纯模形式（如调和Maass形式）引起的兴趣重新兴起，特别是Imamoğlu、Raum和Richter在2014年发展的全纯投影理论产生了许多这样的公式，并被Duncan、Griffin和Ono用于证明Umbral Moonshine猜想。然而，这一理论仅覆盖Pell方程m²-Dn²=N中D为平方数的情形（解有限），需要将其推广到一般D的情形（Pell方程可能有无穷多解）。

Method: 作者发展了一种新的、对许多自然情形更为方便的全纯投影方法，将Imamoğlu、Raum和Richter的理论从D为平方数（Pell方程仅有有限解）的情形推广到一般D（包括Pell方程有无穷多解的情形）。该方法通过对向量值调和Maass形式与全纯模形式乘积的全纯投影进行处理，并将结果系统化地推导出卷积恒等式。

Result: 作为应用，作者解决了第二作者近期提出的一个猜想。更一般地，该方法产生了18个关于mock theta函数的卷积恒等式，并从中提炼出5个可直接用于推导其余恒等式的核心恒等式。此外，附录中还展示了如何通过更直接的q-级数方法证明这些恒等式，但全纯投影公式的关键效用在于它能自动发现和验证此类公式。

Conclusion: 全纯投影公式提供了一种强大的自动化工具，不仅能推导和验证卷积恒等式，而且其推广到一般D的情形大大拓展了应用范围，尤其适用于Pell方程有无穷多解的情况，为解决相关猜想和发现新的mock theta函数恒等式提供了系统性的方法论。

Abstract: Convolution identities and recursive formulas have long played a role in the theory of holomorphic modular forms and their applications. These have served both as striking formulas and as fundamentally useful tools for applications to combinatorics. Recently, there has been renewed interest in examples arising from non-holomorphic modular forms. These include the famous Hurwitz-Kronecker class number relations dating to 1885, and groundbreaking work of Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter from 2014. Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter developed a theory of holomorphic projection for products of (vector-valued) harmonic Maass forms and holomorphic modular forms to produce many such formulas. This was related shortly thereafter by Duncan, Griffin, and Ono to replicable-type functions in the sense of Conway and Norton, and such recursions played a key role in their proof of the Umbral Moonshine Conjecture.
  Here, we develop new results on holomorphic projections of such functions which is more convenient for many natural cases. Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter's choice corresponds to the case in which the generalized Pell equation $m^2 - Dn^2 = N$ has a square value for $D$, which has only finitely many solutions for a given value of $N$. Our main results cover the cases of general $D$, in particular those for which the Pell equation has infinitely many solutions.
  As an application, we resolve a recent conjecture of the second author. More generally, our approach yields 18 convolution identities for mock theta functions, which we boil down to 5 identities that can directly be used to prove the others. In the appendices, we also show how these identities can be proven by more direct $q$-series methods; however, the key utility of the holomorphic projection formulas is that they give a tool to automatically discover and verify such formulas.

</details>


### [130] [Gross vectors modulo 2 and elliptic curves of prime conductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13371)
*Matija Kazalicki,Siniša Slijepčević*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了超奇异椭圆曲线在F_2上的Gross向量奇偶性张成定理，并由此证明了Kazalicki-Kohen猜想和Watkins猜想在特定情形下的成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望理解超奇异椭圆曲线的Brandt特征向量的奇偶性结构，以及其与椭圆曲线算术（Mordell-Weil秩、模度）之间的深层联系。Kazalicki和Kohen猜想指出正秩椭圆曲线在素数导子情况下其Brandt特征向量所有系数均为偶数，这一猜想若成立将提供一种纯代数的秩零判别法。

Method: 关键步骤是将Gross三元格的表示数奇偶性约化为与Frobenius垂直的秩二子格上的表示问题。利用Ibukiyama的显式极大阶，得到的本原二元型被识别为Xiao-Zhou-Deng-Qu参数化中出现的那些。随后通过类域论和Chebotarev定理将单个超奇异坐标分离出来。结合Mestre和Gross-Kudla的公式处理模度整除性问题。

Result: 证明了Gross向量(m_i(D) mod 2)张成F_2^{S_p}。由此推出：若E/Q有素导子p且正Mordell-Weil秩，则其Brandt特征向量所有系数均为偶数，证实Kazalicki-Kohen猜想。同时证明正秩且根数+1的素导子椭圆曲线的模度被4整除，从而Watkins猜想对所有这些秩2曲线成立。

Conclusion: 奇数系数的Brandt特征向量可以作为有理超奇异类处秩为零的代数证书。本工作将奇偶性、模度整除性与椭圆曲线秩之间建立了深刻联系，推进了椭圆曲线算术研究。

Abstract: Let p > 3 be a prime, and let S_p denote the geometric isomorphism classes of supersingular elliptic curves in characteristic p whose j-invariants lie in F_p. For each negative fundamental discriminant -D for which p is inert in Q(sqrt(-D)), let m_i(D), i in S_p, be the integral coefficients of the corresponding Gross vector. We prove that the vectors (m_i(D) mod 2)_{i in S_p} span F_2^{S_p}. The key step reduces the parity of the representation numbers of Gross's ternary lattices to representation by rank-two sublattices perpendicular to Frobenius. Using Ibukiyama's explicit maximal orders, the resulting primitive binary forms are identified with those occurring in the Xiao--Zhou--Deng--Qu parametrization of supersingular elliptic curves over F_p. Class field theory and Chebotarev's theorem then allow the individual supersingular coordinates to be isolated.
  As a consequence, if E/Q has prime conductor p and positive Mordell--Weil rank, then every coefficient of its Brandt eigenvector indexed by S_p is even, proving a conjecture of Kazalicki and Kohen. Thus an odd coefficient at a rational supersingular class is an algebraic certificate of rank 0. Combining this parity theorem with formulas of Mestre and Gross--Kudla, we also prove that the modular degree of every positive-rank elliptic curve of prime conductor and root number +1 is divisible by 4. Consequently, Watkins' conjecture holds for all such curves of rank 2.

</details>


### [131] [Intersective Polynomials and Universal Separation of Divosor Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13388)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对互素多项式对以及至多二次的多项式对，给出了"除数型映射d_A(F(n))与d_A(G(n))之间普遍无界分离"的完整分类。核心结论是：普遍无界分离当且仅当F或G之一是"相交型"(intersective)多项式（即模每个正整数都有根）；对具有公共不可约因子的对，分离条件由UV的相交性以及Galois作用无不相交点等算术性质刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在对任意次数互素多项式对以及至多二次的所有多项式对，给出除数型乘法函数沿多项式取值时的普遍分离准则的完整分类。此前仅对特殊情形（如线性多项式、特定二次型）有部分刻画，作者希望填充分离理论中关于多项式在除数函数作用下的普遍无界分离的间隙，特别是处理具有公共因子的多项式对时的p-adic边界情形。

Method: 本文采用组合数论与代数数论相结合的方法。关键工具包括：(1) 在根算术级数上获得均匀的几乎素数值的论证，用于构造足够多可被A中元素整除的输入；(2) 自适应的局部路由(adaptive local-routing)论证，用于处理模p-幂条件下的主因子分配问题；(3) 利用Galois作用中无不相交点(derangement)的性质来刻画UV模几乎所有素数有根的条件；(4) 对次数至多2的情形，将有限多个p-adic边界情形逐一消解。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 对于互素非零多项式对F,G，d_A(F(n))与d_A(G(n))之间普遍无界分离当且仅当F,G之一为相交型多项式；(2) 一般地，一个相交型因子可以同时对所有有限多个"对手"多项式产生单边支配；(3) 具有公共不可约因子时，普遍分离迫使UV模几乎所有素数有根，这等价于其Galois作用不含不相交点；(4) 对所有次数至多2的多项式对，分离成立当且仅当UV与F,G中至少一个是相交型的，且该判据不受常数因子(content)或因子重数影响；(5) 对任意次数情形，当涉及三个线性支撑因子且重数为任意正整数时，同一判据成立。

Conclusion: 论文完成了多项式对在除数型函数普遍分离问题上的系统性分类。对互素多项式对给出了简洁而完整的充分必要条件（相交性）；对非互素情形建立了UV的局部根条件作为普遍分离的充要条件，并完全解决了次数至多2情形以及三个线性支撑因子的任意次数情形。证明方法的组合创新（自适应局部路由与均匀几乎素数值的结合）为今后研究高次数的非互素情形提供了技术路线。

Abstract: We classify universal divisor-profile separation for coprime polynomial pairs of arbitrary degree and for all pairs of degree at most two. For \(A\subset\N\) and \(m\in\Z\), let \(d_A(m)\) count the members of \(A\) dividing \(m\). For coprime nonzero \(F,G\in\Z[x]\), universally unbounded separation between \(d_A(F(n))\) and \(d_A(G(n))\) occurs if and only if one of \(F,G\) is intersective, that is, has a root modulo every positive integer. More generally, an intersective factor separated from finitely many polynomial opponents yields simultaneous one-sided dominance against all of them.
  For pairs with common irreducible factors, let \(U,V\) be the products of the factors occurring only on the two respective sides. Universal separation forces \(UV\) to have a root modulo almost every prime; equivalently, its Galois action has no derangement. We resolve the remaining finite \(p\)-adic boundary for all pairs of degree at most two: separation holds exactly when \(UV\) and at least one of \(F,G\) are intersective, and the criterion is unchanged by contents or factor multiplicities. The same criterion holds, in arbitrary degree, for three linear support factors with arbitrary positive multiplicities. The proofs combine uniform almost-prime values on root progressions with an adaptive local-routing argument.

</details>


### [132] [The distribution of $k$-free ideals in ray class groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13509)
*Adrian Barquero-Sanchez,Jack Heimrath,Bernd Sing,Nicolás Sirolli,Caylee Spivey,Michael Wijaya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将从经典的k-free整数在算术级数中分布问题推广到任意数域的设定，利用射线类群的语言，建立了有界范数的k-free理想在给定射线类中数量的渐近公式（含误差项），证明了k-free理想在射线类中的等分布性质，并给出了具体计算示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典问题——k-free整数在算术级数中的分布已有Gegenbauer和Cohen-Robinson的研究成果，先前工作如Benkowski、Nymann和Sittinger已将此类问题推广到数域的理想类群和窄类群，但尚未覆盖更一般的射线类群设定。本文旨在将该问题推广到任意数域的射线类群框架下，建立统一的渐近理论。

Method: 利用射线类群（ray class groups）的语言描述数域中的算术级数类比，通过分析k-free理想的计数函数，建立渐近公式及误差项。在理想类群和窄类群的特殊情形下，运用Landau关于理想计数的更精确渐近估计来改进误差项。借助Dirichlet L-函数和类群特征等解析数论工具推导等分布性质。

Result: 建立了k-free理想在有界范数下分布于各射线类中的渐近公式和误差项，证明了k-free理想在射线类中渐近等分布。在理想类群和窄类群情形下获得改进的误差估计。恢复了有理数域上的经典公式，并将先前研究推广到射线类群框架，同时给出数值计算示例验证渐近行为。

Conclusion: 本文完成了k-free整数分布问题在任意数域射线类群框架下的系统推广，建立了完整的渐近理论和等分布结果，统一并推广了此前的多个研究成果。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend the classical problem of studying the distribution of $k$-free integers in arithmetic progressions to the setting of arbitrary number fields. Using the language of ray class groups, we establish asymptotic formulas, together with error terms, for the number of $k$-free ideals of bounded norm lying in a given ray class. In particular, our results show that $k$-free ideals are equidistributed among ray classes. We also obtain improved error estimates in the cases of ideal class groups and narrow class groups by using sharper ideal counting asymptotics due to Landau. Our results recover the classical formulas of Gegenbauer and Cohen--Robinson over $\mathbb{Q}$ and extend previous work of Benkowski, Nymann, and Sittinger to the setting of ray class groups. We also present explicit computational examples that illustrate the asymptotic formulas and the equidistribution of $k$-free ideals among ray classes.

</details>

### [133] [Nilpotent orbits of sl(n,R) admit no Gibbs state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12608)
*Jérémie Pierard de Maujouy,Guillaume Neuttiens*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了SL(n,R)的幂零轨道上的Gibbs态存在性问题，证明了当n>2时，这些幂零轨道不承认任何Gibbs态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs态是定义在Hamiltonian G-流形上的概率分布，由李代数g中的开集参数化，是系统在G作用下的自然平衡态。研究幂零轨道上是否存在Gibbs态，对于理解这些轨道上的平衡态结构和动力学性质具有重要意义。

Method: 本文通过分析SL(n,R)的幂零轨道结构，利用Gibbs态的定义和性质（由李代数g的开集参数化），对n>2的情形进行了系统的存在性论证，证明幂零轨道上不存在任何Gibbs态。

Result: 证明了对于SL(n,R)，当n>2时，其幂零轨道不承认任何Gibbs态。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一个否定的存在性结果：SL(n,R)（n>2）的幂零轨道上不存在Gibbs态，这揭示了这类轨道在平衡态理论中的特殊地位。

Abstract: Gibbs states are probability distributions defined on Hamiltonian G-manifolds. They are parametrized by an open set of the Lie algebra g and arise as natural equilibrium states with regard to the action of G on the system. In this paper, we focus on nilpotent orbits of SL(n,R). We show that for n>2, those orbits do not admit any Gibbs state.

</details>


### [134] [Minimal Nilpotent Orbits of type G2, F4 and E8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12985)
*Boming Jia*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于类型为G₂、F₄、E₈的复单李代数，其极小幂零轨道闭包不能同构于任何光滑拟仿射簇X的余切丛的仿射化(T*X)^aff，且不要求同构是等变的或Poisson的。在类型A₁和A₂中也证明了类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究幂零轨道闭包与余切丛仿射化之间的同构关系，这是辛几何与表示论交叉领域的重要问题。先前的结果可能依赖于等变或Poisson同构假设，本文试图在更弱的非等变、非Poisson假设下证明这些类型的最小幂零轨道闭包不具有这种结构。

Method: 针对复单李代数G₂、F₄、E₈类型（以及A₁、A₂类型），通过分析极小幂零轨道闭包的几何和奇点性质，排除其与任何光滑拟仿射簇X的余切丛仿射化(T*X)^aff同构的可能性。方法不依赖等变或Poisson结构的保持。

Result: 证明了在G₂、F₄、E₈类型中，最小幂零轨道闭包不同构于任何光滑拟仿射簇余切丛的仿射化；在A₁和A₂类型中也验证了相同的结论。

Conclusion: 这些类型的极小幂零轨道闭包具有某种内在的几何性质（可能与奇点结构或余切丛仿射化的全局几何特征不相容），使得它们无法被实现为光滑拟仿射簇余切丛的仿射化，即使在完全不假设等变或Poisson结构的宽松条件下也是如此。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak g$ be a complex simple Lie algebra of type $G_2$, $F_4$, or $E_8$. We prove that the closure $\overline{\mathcal O}_{\min}(\mathfrak g)$ of its minimal nilpotent orbit is not isomorphic to $(T^*X)^{\mathrm{aff}}$ for any smooth quasi-affine variety $X$. We do not assume that the isomorphism is equivariant or Poisson. We also prove the same statement in types $A_1$ and $A_2$.

</details>


### [135] [Absolutely flat algebras in tensor categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13137)
*Kevin Coulembier,Alexander Sherman*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了张量范畴（刚性阿贝尔幺半范畴）的ind-完备化中半单代数、阿廷单代数和阿廷绝对平坦代数之间的关系，证明了在对中等增长的对称张量范畴中（在主要猜想成立的前提下涵盖所有情况），这三类交换代数是重合的；同时给出反例说明一般情况下这些概念会分歧。附录一首次给出具有有限维态射空间但对象无限长度的张量范畴例子，附录二建立了Deligne定理的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典代数几何和表示论中，域上代数的半单性、阿廷性（Artinian）和绝对平坦性（absolutely flat）之间存在密切关系。作者希望将这些经典概念推广到张量范畴的ind-完备化这一更一般的框架中，探讨在何种条件下这些不同的代数性质仍然保持等价，以及在一般情形下它们会如何分歧。

Method: 作者系统性地比较了张量范畴ind-完备化中三类代数（半单代数、阿廷单代数、阿廷绝对平坦代数）之间的关系。通过限制在中等增长（moderate growth）的对称张量范畴中的交换代数，证明了三者重合；同时构造具体反例展示一般情形下的分歧。附录一构造了具有有限维态射空间但对象无限长度的张量范畴作为反例；附录二推广了Deligne关于tannakian范畴在代数闭域上可中性化的定理。

Result: 主要结果为：在（接受领域主要猜想时）所有中等增长对称张量范畴中，交换代数的三种性质（半单性、阿廷单性、阿廷绝对平坦性）相互等价。此外，通过反例展示了一般张量范畴中这些概念会发生分歧，表明经典域上代数的情形不可直接照搬。附录一的例子回答了关于张量范畴结构的基本问题，附录二给出了Deligne定理的重要推广。

Conclusion: 张量范畴的ind-完备化中代数结构理论在中等增长条件下与经典情形高度一致，但一般情形下性质会退化。这不仅深化了对张量范畴代数结构的理解，也产生了新的反例（无限长度对象）和方法（Deligne定理的推广），对表示论和范畴代数具有潜在的重要应用价值。

Abstract: We study the relation between semisimple algebras, artinian simple algebras, and artinian absolutely flat algebras in ind-completions of tensor categories (rigid abelian monoidal categories). Our main results show that the three types of algebras coincide, if we restrict to commutative algebras in certain (all, if we accept the main conjectures in the field) symmetric tensor categories of moderate growth. We also provide examples to show that in general these notions tend to diverge, contrary to the classical case of algebras over a field. In one appendix we give the first, to the best of our knowledge, example of a tensor category with finite-dimensional morphism spaces but objects of infinite length. In a second appendix we establish a generalisation of Deligne's theorem that tannakian categories are neutral over algebraically closed fields.

</details>


### [136] [$g$-vector fans and picture categories for 0-Auslander extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13175)
*Erlend D. Børve,Maximilian Kaipel*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将g-向量扇的概念推广到带投射silting对象的Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴上，证明了其具有由厚子范畴诱导的可容许划分，从而可以定义picture category，并建立了厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射，最后引入划分扇态射统一了不同代数与范畴之间的函子关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往g-向量扇和picture category的构造局限于有限维代数或特定范畴类，缺乏统一框架。作者希望将g-向量扇的概念推广到更一般的extriangulated范畴（包含三角范畴和正合范畴的统一框架），并建立picture category与τ-cluster morphism category之间统一的构造方法，同时用态射统一不同对象之间的函子关系。

Method: 作者首先在Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴中对带投射silting对象T定义g-向量扇；然后利用厚子范畴诱导该扇的可容许划分（依第二位作者的定义），从而构造picture category；接着建立由presilting对象生成的且包含所有投射-内射对象的厚子范畴与T的端射k-代数的τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射；最后引入划分扇的态射概念，为不同代数和范畴的picture category之间的函子关系提供统一框架。

Result: 证明了推广后的g-向量扇具有可容许划分，建立了厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴的一一对应，表明该构造统一了有限维代数上所有先前的picture category和τ-cluster morphism category的构造，并通过划分扇态射统一了它们之间的函子关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的理论框架，将g-向量扇、picture category和τ-cluster morphism category的构造推广到extriangulated范畴的一般设定中，并通过划分扇态射提供了不同对象间函子关系的统一描述。

Abstract: We extend the notion of $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan so that it is defined for a Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander $k$-linear extriangulated category $\mathcal{C}$ with a projective silting object $T$. Moreover, we show that the $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan admits an admissible partition, in the sense of the second-named author, which is induced by thick subcategories. One can thus define the picture category of $\mathcal{C}$. We establish a bijection between thick subcategories of $\mathcal{C}$ generated by presilting objects containing all projective-injective objects and $τ$-perpendicular subcategories of the endomorphism $k$-algebra of $T$. This shows that our construction unifies all previous constructions of picture categories and $τ$-cluster morphism categories of finite-dimensional algebras. We introduce morphisms of partitioned fans to provide a common framework for the functorial relationships between picture categories of different algebras and categories.

</details>


### [137] [Homogeneous Weights on Semigroup Algebras of Finite Commutative Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13180)
*M. H. Shahzamanian*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上扭曲收缩半群代数的行列式，将Steinberg的行列式理论推广到该设定，建立Frobenius判据，并发展双生成扭曲收缩幺半群代数的结构理论，进而构造Frobenius扭曲收缩半群代数上的齐次权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限半群的行列式与其半群代数的Frobenius性质密切相关，且在编码理论中有重要应用。作者旨在将已有的行列式理论推广到扭曲收缩半群代数这一更一般的设定中，并在此基础上构造齐次权重。

Method: 论文首先建立了扭曲收缩半群代数在有限域上的Frobenius判据，推广了Steinberg的行列式理论。随后发展了双生成扭曲收缩幺半群代数的结构理论，识别出矩阵行列式仅依赖于零-非零模式而不依赖上圈取值的类。基于特征理论构造齐次权重的思路，作者构造了Frobenius扭曲收缩半群代数上的齐次权重，并在非Frobenius情形下刻画了使特征平均权重满足齐次权重平均性质的诸主理想。

Result: 论文识别了一类半群，其行列式条件只需对平凡特征验证即可推广到所有特征。在适当假设下，这些半群可由少数定义恒等式完全确定。此外，作者将经典齐次权重概念推广到特定的主理想族上，刻画了相应的主理想特征。

Conclusion: 本文成功将行列式理论与Frobenius判据推广至扭曲收缩半群代数，并拓展了齐次权重的构造范围，为编码理论中有限Frobenius环上的权重设计提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The determinant of a finite semigroup is closely related to the Frobenius property of its corresponding semigroup algebra, and it plays a significant role in coding theory applications. In this paper, we study determinants of twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We extend Steinberg's determinant theory to this setting by establishing a Frobenius criterion for twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We also develop a structural theory for two-generated twisted contracted monoid algebras. In particular, we identify classes for which the determinant of the associated matrix depends only on its zero--nonzero pattern, and is therefore independent of the values of the cocycle. Consequently, it suffices to verify the determinant condition for the trivial character, from which the corresponding result for all characters follows. Under suitable assumptions, these semigroups are completely determined by a small set of defining identities. Motivated by the character-theoretic construction of homogeneous weights on finite Frobenius rings, we construct homogeneous weights for Frobenius twisted contracted semigroup algebras and investigate the non-Frobenius case. In particular, we characterize the principal ideals on which the character-average weight satisfies the averaging property of homogeneous weights and thereby extend the classical notion of homogeneous weight to a prescribed family of principal ideals.

</details>


### [138] [Categorification of the genus two DAHA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13278)
*Semeon Arthamonov,Ievgen Makedonskyi,Daniil Sarafannikov*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了平坦退化D(2|1,α)当前代数的分级可积表示的导出范畴上的三个导出自函子，证明其在Grothendieck群中的类为亏格2的Macdonald算子。通过显式计算分级Ext配对，推导出亏格2 Macdonald算子的自伴性以及亏格2 Macdonald多项式关于特定斜双线性形式的正交性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在将Macdonald算子的构造从经典李代数（如仿射李代数）推广到超代数D(2|1,α)的当前代数（current algebra）的平坦退化情形，从而在更广泛的代数结构中建立与Macdonald理论（对称函数论中的重要对象）之间的联系，并揭示这些算子的内在几何与代数性质。

Method: 作者通过构造当前代数的三个派生自函子（derived endofunctors），并利用Grothendieck群中的类与亏格2 Macdonald算子建立对应关系。随后，通过显式计算导出范畴中的分级Ext配对（graded Ext pairing），分析这些算子的自伴性，并进一步研究相应的Macdonald多项式的正交性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 三个派生自函子在Grothendieck群中的类恰好是亏格2的Macdonald算子；(2) 通过分级Ext配对的显式计算，证明了亏格2 Macdonald算子的自伴性；(3) 推导出亏格2 Macdonald多项式在某个斜双线性形式下满足正交性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了当前代数的表示论与亏格2 Macdonald理论之间的深层联系，给出了亏格2 Macdonald算子的一种代数表示论构造，并证明了其关键的正交性与自伴性性质，为更高亏格的Macdonald理论推广提供了新的途径。

Abstract: We construct three derived endofunctors on the derived category of graded integrable representations of the current algebra of a flat degeneration of $D(2|1,α)$, and prove that their classes in the Grothendieck group are the genus two Macdonald operators. Computing the graded $\operatorname{Ext}$ pairing explicitly, we deduce the self-adjointness of the genus two Macdonald operators and the orthogonality of genus $2$ Macdonald polynomials with respect to the certain skew-bilinear form.

</details>


### [139] [Moduli of super-representations of quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13375)
*Sanjay Amrutiya,Umesh Dubey*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了King的几何不变量理论（GIT）框架到超设置，构造了超表示箭图的模空间，并概述了超箭图簇的构造，将超格拉斯曼流形和超旗簇实现为几何商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将King的GIT框架推广到超表示范畴，以构造参数化超维数固定的超表示模空间，并进一步建立超箭图簇以及与超格拉斯曼流形、超旗簇的联系。

Method: 利用超一般线性群的偶部分和奇偶移位算子，扩展King的GIT商构造到超设置；对带框架的箭图（不进行加倍）构造超箭图簇；通过几何商实现超格拉斯曼流形和超旗簇。

Result: 成功构造了在拓扑意义上参数化固定超维数超表示的模空间，建立了超箭图簇的构造框架，并将超格拉斯曼流形和超旗簇统一在此框架下的几何商表述中。

Conclusion: 该工作为超表示论提供了模空间构造的基础工具，拓展了GIT方法在超几何中的应用，并为后续超箭图簇理论的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this note, we construct moduli spaces of super-representations of quivers by extending King's Geometric Invariant Theory (GIT) framework to the super setting. Using the even part of super general linear groups and a parity-shifting operator, we construct moduli spaces that topologically parameterize super-representations of a fixed super-dimension.
  We also outline the construction of super quiver varieties for super-representations of framed quivers (without doubling); and realise super-Grassmannians and super-flag varieties as geometric quotients within this setting.

</details>


### [140] [Langlands Duality and Invariant Differential Operators: the Case SL(2n+1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13400)
*V. K. Dobrev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文探讨Langlands对偶与不变微分算子之间的联系，研究SL(2n+1)群的情况，补充了之前关于SL(2n)群的工作，试图在这两个长期被认为无关的数学领域之间建立桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Langlands纲领是数学研究中最有影响力的课题之一，而不变微分算子这一主题此前似乎与Langlands纲领无关。由于两者都深深植根于Harish-Chandra关于半单李群表示论的工作，作者认为它们之间存在被忽视的联系，并试图建立这种桥梁。

Method: 作者首先研究了SL(2n)群的情形，在本文中将研究扩展到SL(2n+1)群。虽然这两个群相似，但它们的表示论存在显著差异，需要针对SL(2n+1)的特殊性进行新的处理。

Result: 本文完成了对SL(2n+1)群情形的分析，揭示了该群的Langlands对偶与不变微分算子之间的具体联系，验证了作者在两类群之间建立桥梁的一般方法。

Conclusion: 通过在SL(2n)和SL(2n+1)两类群上的工作，作者逐步建立了Langlands对偶与不变微分算子之间的桥梁，尽管这两个群的表示论有差异，但联系仍然成立。

Abstract: Recently we started building a bridge between two cases of Langlands duality. The latter is one of the most influential topics in mathematical research. It has many different appearances and influential subtopics. Yet there is a topic that until now seems unrelated to the Langlands program. That is the topic of invariant differential operators. That is strange since both items are deeply rooted in Harish-Chandra's representation theory of semisimple Lie groups.
  We started with the case of the group ~$SL(2n)$. In the present paper we deal with the group $SL(2n+1)$. The two mentioned groups are similar, but their representation theories is rather different.

</details>
